## 1. PROJECTS LIST PAGE (`/projects`)

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/projects/page.tsx`

**Current Display:**
- Table with columns: Name, Description, Recent Releases, Semantic Releases, Created
- Shows top 3 releases/semantic releases per project (expandable with "more..." button)
- Release chips link to `/releases/{id}`
- Semantic release chips link to `/projects/{id}/semantic-releases/{id}`
- Clean header with "New Project" button linking to `/projects/new`

**Data Fetching:**
- Uses `projectsApi.list()` to fetch all projects
- For each project, displays inline:
  - `releasesApi.listByProject(projectId, 1, limit+1)` 
  - `semanticReleases.list(projectId, 1, limit+1)`
  - Provider icons fetched from sources

## 2. PROJECT CREATION FLOW (`/projects/new`)

**Files:**
- Page: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/projects/new/page.tsx`
- Form Component: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/projects/project-form.tsx`

**Current Creation Form (`ProjectForm`):**

The form collects:

1. **Basic Info**
   - `name` (required, text input)
   - `description` (optional, textarea, 2 rows)

2. **Agent Configuration**
   - `agent_prompt` (optional, textarea, 6 rows, monospace)
     - Placeholder: "Custom instructions for the agent when analyzing releases for this project..."
   
3. **Agent Trigger Rules** (in collapsible border section)
   - `on_major_release` (switch toggle, default: true)
   - `on_minor_release` (switch toggle, default: false)
   - `on_security_patch` (switch toggle, default: true)
   - `version_pattern` (optional, text input, regex)
     - Placeholder: `^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$`

**Form States:**
- Loading/saving state: `"Saving..."` button text
- Error display: red alert box with error message
- Cancel button redirects back

**API Call:**
```typescript
projectsApi.create(ProjectInput)
// Sends POST /projects
```

**Redirect:** Success → `/projects`

## 3. PROJECT DETAIL PAGE (`/projects/[id]`)

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/projects/project-detail.tsx`

- "Add Source" button → `/projects/{id}/sources/new`
- Provider badges: Docker Hub (blue), GitHub (black)

### Tab 2: Context Sources
- Table with columns: Type (pill badge), Name, Config URL (truncated), Delete button

### Tab 4: Agent

- **Trigger Rules Section:**

  - Version pattern text input (monospace)
  - Save button for all agent config

- **Run History Section:**
  - Table with columns: Trigger, Status (with StatusDot), Started, Duration, Semantic Release (link)
  - Shows formatted duration (e.g., "2m 30s")

**Data Loading:**
- Lazy loads based on active tab using `useSWR` with null conditions
- Sources, context sources, semantic releases, and agent runs only fetch when tab is active

## 9. API CLIENT TYPES & INTERFACE

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts`

### Project Interface
```typescript
interface Project {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  agent_prompt?: string;
  agent_rules?: AgentRules;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

interface ProjectInput {
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  agent_prompt?: string;
  agent_rules?: AgentRules;
}

interface AgentRules {
  on_major_release?: boolean;
  on_minor_release?: boolean;
  on_security_patch?: boolean;
  version_pattern?: string;
}
```

### Source Interface
```typescript
interface Source {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  provider: string;
  repository: string;
  poll_interval_seconds: number;
  enabled: boolean;
  config?: Record<string, unknown>;
  last_polled_at?: string;
  last_error?: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

interface SourceInput {
  provider: string;
  repository: string;
  poll_interval_seconds: number;
  enabled: boolean;
  config?: Record<string, unknown>;
}
```

### Release Interface
```typescript
interface Release {
  id: string;
  source_id: string;
  version: string;
  raw_data?: Record<string, unknown>;
  released_at?: string;
  created_at: string;
}
```

### Context Source Interface
```typescript
interface ContextSource {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  type: string;
  name: string;
  config: Record<string, unknown>;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

interface ContextSourceInput {
  type: string;
  name: string;
  config: Record<string, unknown>;
}
```

### Semantic Release Interface
```typescript
interface SemanticRelease {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  version: string;
  report?: SemanticReport;
  status: string;
  error?: string;
  created_at: string;
  completed_at?: string;
}

interface SemanticReport {
  summary: string;
  availability: string;
  adoption: string;
  urgency: string;
  recommendation: string;
}
```

### Agent Run Interface
```typescript
interface AgentRun {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  semantic_release_id?: string;
  trigger: string;
  status: string;
  prompt_used?: string;
  error?: string;
  started_at?: string;
  completed_at?: string;
  created_at: string;
}
```

## 10. API CLIENT METHODS

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts`

```typescript
// Projects
projects = {
  list: (page = 1, perPage = 25) => GET /projects?page={page}&per_page={perPage}
  get: (id: string) => GET /projects/{id}
  create: (input: ProjectInput) => POST /projects
  update: (id: string, input: ProjectInput) => PUT /projects/{id}
  delete: (id: string) => DELETE /projects/{id}
}

// Sources
sources = {
  listByProject: (projectId: string, page = 1) => GET /projects/{projectId}/sources?page={page}
  create: (projectId: string, input: SourceInput) => POST /projects/{projectId}/sources
  get: (id: string) => GET /sources/{id}
  update: (id: string, input: SourceInput) => PUT /sources/{id}
  delete: (id: string) => DELETE /sources/{id}
}

// Releases
releases = {
  list: (page = 1, perPage = 25) => GET /releases?page={page}&per_page={perPage}
  listBySource: (sourceId: string, page = 1) => GET /sources/{sourceId}/releases?page={page}
  listByProject: (projectId: string, page = 1, perPage = 25) => GET /projects/{projectId}/releases
  get: (id: string) => GET /releases/{id}
}

// Context Sources
contextSources = {
  list: (projectId: string, page = 1) => GET /projects/{projectId}/context-sources?page={page}
  create: (projectId: string, input: ContextSourceInput) => POST /projects/{projectId}/context-sources
  get: (id: string) => GET /context-sources/{id}
  update: (id: string, input: ContextSourceInput) => PUT /context-sources/{id}
  delete: (id: string) => DELETE /context-sources/{id}
}

// Semantic Releases
semanticReleases = {
  list: (projectId: string, page = 1, perPage = 25) => GET /projects/{projectId}/semantic-releases
  get: (id: string) => GET /semantic-releases/{id}
}

// Agent
agent = {
  triggerRun: (projectId: string) => POST /projects/{projectId}/agent/run
  listRuns: (projectId: string, page = 1) => GET /projects/{projectId}/agent/runs?page={page}
  getRun: (id: string) => GET /agent-runs/{id}
}
```

## 11. KEY UI COMPONENTS

### Provider Badge
- Docker Hub: blue background (#2496ed) with Docker icon
- GitHub: black background (#1a1a1a) with GitHub icon
- Shows label text below icon

### Version Chip
- Light gray background (#f3f3f1)
- Monospace font (JetBrains Mono)
- Small padding, rounded corners

### Status Dot
- Colored circle indicator for status visualization

### Section Label
- Used for heading sections within a card
- Bold, serif font

### Urgency Callout
- Only shows for HIGH or CRITICAL urgency
- CSS-styled alert box with icon
- High: amber/orange styling
- Critical: red styling

## 12. STYLING & TYPOGRAPHY

**Fonts:**
- **Fraunces** (serif): Page titles, headings, emphasis
- **DM Sans** (sans-serif): Body text, labels, UI controls
- **JetBrains Mono**: Version numbers, repository names, code values

**Brand Colors:**
- Primary Orange: `#e8601a`
- Dark Text: `#111113`
- Muted Text: `#9ca3af`
- Light Border: `#e8e8e5`
- Background: `#ffffff`, `#fafaf9`
- Alert Red: `#dc2626`
- Success Green: `#16a34a`

**Layout:**
- Sidebar (52px collapsed, 200px expanded) + Main content (flex-1)
- Cards with border + padding
- Tables with striped headers
- 6px padding on main content area

## 13. CURRENT STATE SUMMARY

**Project Creation Flow (Current):**
1. User clicks "New Project" button on `/projects`
2. Navigate to `/projects/new`
3. Fill ProjectForm: name, description, agent_prompt, agent_rules
4. Submit → POST /projects
5. Redirect to `/projects`
6. User must manually add sources via "Add Source" in project detail

**Project Onboarding (Current Gaps):**
- No wizard/multi-step flow
- Sources must be added separately after project creation
- Context sources must be added manually
- No guided setup experience
- No success/confirmation messaging

> AGENT

All three decisions are clear. Let me now propose the overall approach.

## Approach: Focused UI Redesign

There's really one clean approach here since the decisions are well-defined. Here's what I recommend:

**Three changes, one coherent redesign:**

1. **Projects page** — Replace the current table with dashboard-style project cards. Each card has three sections: header (name/desc/actions), sources (list with status + inline add form), and recent releases (timeline with source attribution).

2. **Project creation form** — Remove agent prompt and agent rules fields. Replace them with an optional "Add a Source" section (provider, repository, poll interval). The API flow becomes: create project → if source fields filled, also create source.

3. **Project detail page** — Keep mostly as-is, since the dashboard cards surface the key info. The agent config stays in the detail page's Agent tab where it belongs as an advanced feature.

**What stays the same:**
- All existing API endpoints (no backend changes needed)
- Project detail page structure (tabs for sources, context sources, semantic releases, agent)
- Source edit/delete flows
- The rest of the app

**Trade-offs:**
- Dashboard cards take more vertical space than table rows — but they show far more useful info
- Inline source form adds UI state complexity to the projects page — but avoids navigation friction
- The creation flow is two API calls (create project, then create source) — but this is simpler than a transactional endpoint

Does this approach sound right? If so, I'll present the detailed design section by section.

> AGENT

Let me present the detailed design now.

---

## Design Section 1: Projects Page (Dashboard Cards)

**Layout:** Vertical stack of project cards, each card containing three sections.

### Card Header
- Project name (Fraunces serif, clickable → links to project detail)
- Description text (muted, one line)
- Right-aligned action buttons: "Edit" link, overflow menu or delete

### Sources Section
- Section label "Sources" with a `[+ Add Source]` button on the right
- Each source shown as a compact row:
  - Provider icon (GitHub octopus / Docker whale)
  - Repository name (monospace, JetBrains Mono)
  - Status dot (green = active/enabled, gray = disabled)
  - Poll interval (small, muted)
- If no sources: empty state text "No sources configured" with prominent `[+ Add Source]` button
- **Inline add form:** Clicking `[+ Add Source]` expands a form within the card (provider dropdown, repository input, poll interval input, Add button). Cancel collapses it. On submit, calls `sourcesApi.create()` and refreshes via SWR mutate.

### Recent Releases Section
- Section label "Recent Releases"
- Shows the latest 3-5 releases across all sources, sorted by date
- Each release row: version chip (monospace), provider icon, source repo name (truncated), relative time ("2h ago")
- If no releases: "No releases yet" muted text
- "View all" link → project detail page

### Data Fetching
- Fetch all projects via `projectsApi.list()`
- For each project, fetch sources via `sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)`
- For each project, fetch releases via `releasesApi.listByProject(projectId, 1, 5)`
- Use SWR for caching and revalidation

Does this section look right so far?

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

## Design Section 2: Project Creation Form

**Route:** `/projects/new` (same URL, redesigned form)

### Form Fields

1. **Name** (required, text input) — unchanged
2. **Description** (optional, textarea) — unchanged
3. **Source section** (optional, collapsible, labeled "Add a Source")
   - **Provider** dropdown: "GitHub" or "Docker Hub"
   - **Repository** text input, placeholder: "e.g. ethereum/go-ethereum"
   - **Poll Interval** number input, default 300, minimum 60, suffix "seconds"

### What's Removed
- Agent prompt textarea — gone entirely from creation
- Agent trigger rules (on_major_release, on_minor_release, on_security_patch, version_pattern) — gone entirely from creation
- These remain accessible in the project detail page's Agent tab

### Submit Flow
1. Call `projectsApi.create({ name, description })` — no agent fields
2. If source fields are filled: call `sourcesApi.create(newProjectId, { provider, repository, poll_interval_seconds, enabled: true })`
3. If source creation fails: show error but project is already created — redirect to project detail with a toast/warning
4. On full success: redirect to `/projects`

### Form Component Changes
- Modify `ProjectForm` component to accept a `mode` prop or just restructure it
- Remove agent prompt and agent rules from the form entirely
- Add optional source fields section

Does this section look right?

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

## Design Section 3: Project Detail Page Adjustments

**Minimal changes here** — the detail page already works well. Just a few tweaks for consistency:

### What stays the same
- All four tabs (Sources, Context Sources, Semantic Releases, Agent)
- Agent tab keeps agent prompt and trigger rules editing — this is where advanced config lives
- Source/context source management within tabs
- Semantic release detail view

### What changes
- **Edit project form** (`/projects/[id]/edit`): Also remove agent prompt and agent rules from the edit form, since those are managed in the Agent tab. The edit form should only have name and description — matching the simplified creation form.

That's it for this section — the detail page is already well-structured, and the dashboard cards on the projects page solve the discoverability problem without needing to change the detail page itself.

Does this look right?

> DEVELOPER

yes

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
     5→import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
     6→import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
     7→import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
     8→import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
     9→import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
    10→import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
    11→import type { Project, ProjectInput } from "@/lib/api/types";
    12→
    13→interface ProjectFormProps {
    14→  initial?: Project;
    15→  onSubmit: (input: ProjectInput) => Promise<void>;
    16→  title: string;
    17→}
    18→
    19→export function ProjectForm({ initial, onSubmit, title }: ProjectFormProps) {
    20→  const router = useRouter();
    21→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    22→  const [name, setName] = useState(initial?.name ?? "");
    23→  const [description, setDescription] = useState(initial?.description ?? "");
    24→  const [agentPrompt, setAgentPrompt] = useState(initial?.agent_prompt ?? "");
    25→  const [onMajor, setOnMajor] = useState(initial?.agent_rules?.on_major_release ?? true);
    26→  const [onMinor, setOnMinor] = useState(initial?.agent_rules?.on_minor_release ?? false);
    27→  const [onSecurity, setOnSecurity] = useState(initial?.agent_rules?.on_security_patch ?? true);
    28→  const [versionPattern, setVersionPattern] = useState(initial?.agent_rules?.version_pattern ?? "");
    29→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    30→
    31→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    32→    e.preventDefault();
    33→    setError("");
    34→    setSaving(true);
    35→    try {
    36→      await onSubmit({
    37→        name,
    38→        description: description || undefined,
    39→        agent_prompt: agentPrompt || undefined,
    40→        agent_rules: {
    41→          on_major_release: onMajor,
    42→          on_minor_release: onMinor,
    43→          on_security_patch: onSecurity,
    44→          version_pattern: versionPattern || undefined,
    45→        },
    46→      });
    47→      router.push("/projects");
    48→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    49→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save");
    50→    } finally {
    51→      setSaving(false);
    52→    }
    53→  };
    54→
    55→  return (
    56→    <Card className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
    57→      <CardHeader>
    58→        <CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle>
    59→      </CardHeader>
    60→      <CardContent>
    61→        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
    62→          {error && <div className="rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>}
    63→          <div className="space-y-2">
    64→            <Label htmlFor="name">Name</Label>
    65→            <Input id="name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} required />
    66→          </div>
    67→          <div className="space-y-2">
    68→            <Label htmlFor="description">Description</Label>
    69→            <Textarea id="description" value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} rows={2} />
    70→          </div>
    71→          <div className="space-y-2">
    72→            <Label htmlFor="agent_prompt">Agent Prompt</Label>
    73→            <Textarea
    74→              id="agent_prompt"
    75→              value={agentPrompt}
    76→              onChange={(e) => setAgentPrompt(e.target.value)}
    77→              rows={6}
    78→              placeholder="Custom instructions for the agent when analyzing releases for this project..."
    79→              className="font-mono text-sm"
    80→            />
    81→          </div>
    82→          <div className="space-y-3">
    83→            <Label>Agent Rules</Label>
    84→            <div className="rounded-md border p-4 space-y-3">
    85→              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
    86→                <Switch checked={onMajor} onCheckedChange={setOnMajor} />
    87→                <Label>Trigger on major releases</Label>
    88→              </div>
    89→              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
    90→                <Switch checked={onMinor} onCheckedChange={setOnMinor} />
    91→                <Label>Trigger on minor releases</Label>
    92→              </div>
    93→              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
    94→                <Switch checked={onSecurity} onCheckedChange={setOnSecurity} />
    95→                <Label>Trigger on security patches</Label>
    96→              </div>
    97→              <div className="space-y-2">
    98→                <Label htmlFor="version_pattern">Version Pattern (regex, optional)</Label>
    99→                <Input
   100→                  id="version_pattern"
   101→                  value={versionPattern}
   102→                  onChange={(e) => setVersionPattern(e.target.value)}
   103→                  placeholder='e.g. ^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$'
   104→                />
   105→              </div>
   106→            </div>
   107→          </div>
   108→          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
   109→            <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.back()}>Cancel</Button>
   110→            <Button type="submit" disabled={saving}>{saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</Button>
   111→          </div>
   112→        </form>
   113→      </CardContent>
   114→    </Card>
   115→  );
   116→}
   117→

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import useSWR from "swr";
     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import {
     7→  projects as projectsApi,
     8→  releases as releasesApi,
     9→  semanticReleases as srApi,
    10→  sources as sourcesApi,
    11→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    12→import { getProviderIcon } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    13→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    14→import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
    15→import type { Project } from "@/lib/api/types";
    16→
    17→const CHUNK_SIZE = 3;
    18→
    19→function ReleaseChips({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
    20→  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(CHUNK_SIZE);
    21→  const { data } = useSWR(
    22→    `projects/${projectId}/releases?limit=${limit + 1}`,
    23→    () => releasesApi.listByProject(projectId, 1, limit + 1)
    24→  );
    25→  const { data: sourcesData } = useSWR(
    26→    `projects/${projectId}/sources`,
    27→    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
    28→  );
    29→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
    30→  const hasMore = items.length > limit;
    31→  const shown = items.slice(0, limit);
    32→
    33→  const providerMap = new Map<string, string>();
    34→  for (const s of sourcesData?.data ?? []) {
    35→    providerMap.set(s.id, s.provider);
    36→  }
    37→
    38→  if (!data) return <span className="text-[12px] text-[#c4c4c0] italic">loading…</span>;
    39→  if (shown.length === 0) return <span className="text-[12px] text-[#c4c4c0]">—</span>;
    40→
    41→  return (
    42→    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
    43→      {shown.map((r) => {
    44→        const provider = providerMap.get(r.source_id);
    45→        const Icon = provider ? getProviderIcon(provider) : undefined;
    46→        return (
    47→          <Link
    48→            key={r.id}
    49→            href={`/releases/${r.id}`}
    50→            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none text-[#374151] transition-colors hover:bg-[#e8e8e5]"
    51→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
    52→          >
    53→            {Icon && <Icon size={11} className="shrink-0 text-[#6b7280]" />}
    54→            {r.version}
    55→          </Link>
    56→        );
    57→      })}
    58→      {hasMore && (
    59→        <button
    60→          onClick={() => setLimit((n) => n + CHUNK_SIZE)}
    61→          className="text-[11px] text-[#e8601a] hover:underline"
    62→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
    63→        >
    64→          more…
    65→        </button>
    66→      )}
    67→    </div>
    68→  );
    69→}
    70→
    71→function SemanticReleaseChips({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
    72→  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(CHUNK_SIZE);
    73→  const { data } = useSWR(
    74→    `projects/${projectId}/sr?limit=${limit + 1}`,
    75→    () => srApi.list(projectId, 1, limit + 1)
    76→  );
    77→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
    78→  const hasMore = items.length > limit;
    79→  const shown = items.slice(0, limit);
    80→
    81→  if (!data) return <span className="text-[12px] text-[#c4c4c0] italic">loading…</span>;
    82→  if (shown.length === 0) return <span className="text-[12px] text-[#c4c4c0]">—</span>;
    83→
    84→  return (
    85→    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
    86→      {shown.map((sr) => (
    87→        <Link
    88→          key={sr.id}
    89→          href={`/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}
    90→          className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none text-[#1d4ed8] transition-colors hover:bg-[#dbeafe]"
    91→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#eff6ff", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
    92→        >
    93→          {sr.version}
    94→        </Link>
    95→      ))}
    96→      {hasMore && (
    97→        <button
    98→          onClick={() => setLimit((n) => n + CHUNK_SIZE)}
    99→          className="text-[11px] text-[#e8601a] hover:underline"
   100→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   101→        >
   102→          more…
   103→        </button>
   104→      )}
   105→    </div>
   106→  );
   107→}
   108→
   109→function ProjectRow({ project, index }: { project: Project; index: number }) {
   110→  return (
   111→    <tr
   112→      className="transition-colors duration-100 hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   113→      style={index > 0 ? { borderTop: "1px solid #e8e8e5" } : undefined}
   114→    >
   115→      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
   116→        <Link
   117→          href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
   118→          className="font-medium text-[#e8601a] hover:underline"
   119→        >
   120→          {project.name}
   121→        </Link>
   122→      </td>
   123→      <td className="max-w-[220px] truncate px-4 py-2.5 text-[#6b7280]">
   124→        {project.description || "—"}
   125→      </td>
   126→      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
   127→        <ReleaseChips projectId={project.id} />
   128→      </td>
   129→      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
   130→        <SemanticReleaseChips projectId={project.id} />
   131→      </td>
   132→      <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af]">
   133→        {timeAgo(project.created_at)}
   134→      </td>
   135→    </tr>
   136→  );
   137→}
   138→
   139→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   140→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list());
   141→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   142→
   143→  return (
   144→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   145→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   146→        <div>
   147→          <h1
   148→            style={{
   149→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   150→              fontSize: "24px",
   151→              fontWeight: 700,
   152→              color: "#111113",
   153→            }}
   154→          >
   155→            Projects
   156→          </h1>
   157→          <p
   158→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   159→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   160→          >
   161→            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.
   162→          </p>
   163→        </div>
   164→        <Link
   165→          href="/projects/new"
   166→          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   167→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   168→        >
   169→          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   170→          New Project
   171→        </Link>
   172→      </div>
   173→
   174→      <div
   175→        className="overflow-hidden rounded-md"
   176→        style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   177→      >
   178→        {isLoading ? (
   179→          <p
   180→            className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   181→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   182→          >
   183→            Loading…
   184→          </p>
   185→        ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   186→          <p
   187→            className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   188→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   189→          >
   190→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   191→          </p>
   192→        ) : (
   193→          <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}>
   194→            <thead>
   195→              <tr style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   196→                {["Name", "Description", "Recent Releases", "Semantic Releases", "Created"].map(
   197→                  (h) => (
   198→                    <th
   199→                      key={h}
   200→                      className="px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-[#9ca3af]"
   201→                    >
   202→                      {h}
   203→                    </th>
   204→                  )
   205→                )}
   206→              </tr>
   207→            </thead>

   211→              ))}
   212→            </tbody>
   213→          </table>
   214→        )}
   215→      </div>
   216→    </div>
   217→  );
   218→}

     1→// web/lib/api/types.ts
     2→
     3→// --- Response Envelope ---
     4→
     5→export interface ApiResponse<T> {
     6→  data: T;
     7→  meta?: ApiMeta;
     8→  error?: ApiError;
     9→}
    10→
    11→export interface ApiMeta {
    12→  page: number;
    13→  per_page: number;

    15→}

    21→
    22→// --- Domain Models (all IDs are UUID strings) ---
    23→
    24→export interface Project {
    25→  id: string;
    26→  name: string;
    27→  description?: string;
    28→  agent_prompt?: string;

    37→  on_security_patch?: boolean;

    40→

    44→  agent_prompt?: string;
    45→  agent_rules?: AgentRules;
    46→}
    47→

    61→

    68→}
    69→

    78→

    89→export interface ContextSourceInput {

    21→const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "/api/v1";

    23→async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    24→  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    25→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...init?.headers },
    26→    ...init,
    27→  });
    28→  if (!res.ok) {
    29→    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    30→    throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? `Request failed: ${res.status}`);
    31→  }
    32→  return res.json();
    33→}
    34→

     5→const PROVIDER_STYLES: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; label: string; icon: IconType }> = {

    12→}
    13→
    14→interface ProviderBadgeProps {
    15→  provider: string;
    16→  className?: string;

    19→export function ProviderBadge({ provider, className }: ProviderBadgeProps) {

    29→      className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none", className)}
    30→      style={{ backgroundColor: resolved.bg, color: resolved.text }}
    31→    >
    32→      {Icon && <Icon size={11} />}
    33→      {resolved.label}
    34→    </span>
    35→  );
    36→}

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
     5→import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
     6→import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
     7→import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
     8→import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
     9→import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
    10→import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
    11→import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
    12→import type { Source, SourceInput } from "@/lib/api/types";
    13→
    14→interface SourceFormProps {
    15→  initial?: Source;
    16→  projectId?: string;
    17→  onSubmit: (input: SourceInput) => Promise<void>;
    18→  title: string;
    19→  redirectTo?: string;
    20→}
    21→
    22→export function SourceForm({ initial, onSubmit, title, redirectTo }: SourceFormProps) {
    23→  const router = useRouter();
    24→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    25→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    26→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState(initial?.provider ?? "dockerhub");
    27→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState(initial?.repository ?? "");
    28→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState(String(initial?.poll_interval_seconds ?? 300));
    29→  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(initial?.enabled ?? true);
    30→  const [configJson, setConfigJson] = useState(
    31→    JSON.stringify(initial?.config ?? {}, null, 2)
    32→  );
    33→
    34→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    35→    e.preventDefault();
    36→    setError("");
    37→
    38→    let parsedConfig: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    39→    if (configJson.trim() && configJson.trim() !== "{}") {
    40→      try {
    41→        parsedConfig = JSON.parse(configJson);
    42→      } catch {
    43→        setError("Config must be valid JSON");
    44→        return;
    45→      }
    46→    }
    47→
    48→    setSaving(true);
    49→    try {
    50→      await onSubmit({
    51→        provider,
    52→        repository,
    53→        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval),
    54→        enabled,
    55→        config: parsedConfig,
    56→      });
    57→      router.push(redirectTo ?? "/sources");
    58→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    59→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save");
    60→    } finally {
    61→      setSaving(false);
    62→    }
    63→  };
    64→
    65→  return (
    66→    <Card className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
    67→      <CardHeader><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader>
    68→      <CardContent>
    69→        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
    70→          {error && <div className="rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>}
    71→          <div className="space-y-2">
    72→            <Label>Provider</Label>
    73→            <Select value={provider} onValueChange={setProvider}>
    74→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
    75→              <SelectContent>
    76→                <SelectItem value="dockerhub">Docker Hub</SelectItem>
    77→                <SelectItem value="github">GitHub</SelectItem>
    78→              </SelectContent>
    79→            </Select>
    80→          </div>
    81→          <div className="space-y-2">
    82→            <Label htmlFor="repository">Repository</Label>
    83→            <Input id="repository" value={repository} onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. library/golang or ethereum/go-ethereum" required />
    84→          </div>
    85→          <div className="space-y-2">
    86→            <Label htmlFor="poll_interval">Poll Interval (seconds)</Label>
    87→            <Input id="poll_interval" type="number" min={60} value={pollInterval} onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)} />
    88→          </div>
    89→          <div className="space-y-2">
    90→            <Label htmlFor="config">Config (JSON, optional)</Label>
    91→            <Textarea
    92→              id="config"
    93→              value={configJson}
    94→              onChange={(e) => setConfigJson(e.target.value)}
    95→              rows={4}
    96→              className="font-mono text-sm"
    97→              placeholder="{}"
    98→            />
    99→          </div>
   100→          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   101→            <Switch checked={enabled} onCheckedChange={setEnabled} />
   102→            <Label>Enabled</Label>
   103→          </div>
   104→          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
   105→            <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.back()}>Cancel</Button>
   106→            <Button type="submit" disabled={saving}>{saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</Button>
   107→          </div>
   108→        </form>
   109→      </CardContent>
   110→    </Card>
   111→  );
   112→}
   113→

     1→export function timeAgo(dateStr?: string | null): string {
     2→  if (!dateStr) return "\u2014";
     3→  const diff = Date.now() - new Date(dateStr).getTime();
     4→  const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
     5→  if (mins < 60) return `${mins}m ago`;
     6→  const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60);
     7→  if (hrs < 24) return `${hrs}h ago`;
     8→  return `${Math.floor(hrs / 24)}d ago`;
     9→}
    10→

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import useSWR from "swr";
     4→import Link from "next/link";
     5→import { useState } from "react";
     6→import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
     7→import {
     8→  projects as projectsApi,
     9→  sources as sourcesApi,
    10→  contextSources as ctxApi,
    11→  semanticReleases as srApi,
    12→  agent as agentApi,
    13→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    14→import type { AgentRules } from "@/lib/api/types";
    15→import { ProviderBadge } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    16→import { StatusDot } from "@/components/ui/status-dot";
    17→import { VersionChip } from "@/components/ui/version-chip";
    18→import { SectionLabel } from "@/components/ui/section-label";
    19→import { Pencil, Trash2, Play, Plus } from "lucide-react";
    20→
    21→/* ---------- Tabs ---------- */
    22→
    23→const tabs = [
    24→  { key: "sources", label: "Sources" },
    25→  { key: "context", label: "Context Sources" },
    26→  { key: "semantic", label: "Semantic Releases" },
    27→  { key: "agent", label: "Agent" },
    28→] as const;
    29→
    30→type TabKey = (typeof tabs)[number]["key"];
    31→
    32→/* ---------- Helpers ---------- */
    33→
    34→function formatDuration(startedAt?: string, completedAt?: string): string {
    35→  if (!startedAt) return "--";
    36→  const start = new Date(startedAt).getTime();
    37→  const end = completedAt ? new Date(completedAt).getTime() : Date.now();
    38→  const secs = Math.round((end - start) / 1000);
    39→  if (secs < 60) return `${secs}s`;
    40→  const mins = Math.floor(secs / 60);
    41→  const rem = secs % 60;
    42→  return `${mins}m ${rem}s`;
    43→}
    44→
    45→function truncate(str: string, max: number): string {
    46→  return str.length > max ? str.slice(0, max) + "\u2026" : str;
    47→}
    48→
    49→function formatInterval(seconds: number): string {
    50→  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;
    51→  if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.round(seconds / 60)}m`;
    52→  return `${(seconds / 3600).toFixed(1)}h`;
    53→}
    54→
    55→/* ---------- Urgency chip ---------- */
    56→
    57→const URGENCY_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {
    58→  critical: { bg: "#dc2626", text: "#ffffff" },
    59→  high: { bg: "#f97316", text: "#ffffff" },
    60→};
    61→
    62→function UrgencyChip({ urgency }: { urgency: string }) {
    63→  const u = urgency.toLowerCase();
    64→  const style = URGENCY_COLORS[u];
    65→  if (!style) return null;
    66→  return (
    67→    <span
    68→      className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none"
    69→      style={{ backgroundColor: style.bg, color: style.text }}
    70→    >
    71→      {urgency}
    72→    </span>
    73→  );
    74→}
    75→
    76→/* ---------- Main component ---------- */
    77→
    78→export function ProjectDetail({ id }: { id: string }) {
    79→  const router = useRouter();
    80→  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabKey>("sources");
    81→  const [triggering, setTriggering] = useState(false);
    82→
    83→  /* Agent config local state */
    84→  const [promptDraft, setPromptDraft] = useState<string | null>(null);
    85→  const [rulesDraft, setRulesDraft] = useState<AgentRules | null>(null);
    86→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    87→
    88→  /* Data fetching */
    89→  const { data, isLoading, mutate: mutateProject } = useSWR(`project-${id}`, () => projectsApi.get(id));
    90→  const { data: sourcesData, mutate: mutateSources } = useSWR(
    91→    activeTab === "sources" ? `project-${id}-sources` : null,
    92→    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(id),
    93→  );
    94→  const { data: ctxData, mutate: mutateCtx } = useSWR(
    95→    activeTab === "context" ? `project-${id}-ctx` : null,
    96→    () => ctxApi.list(id),
    97→  );
    98→  const { data: srData } = useSWR(
    99→    activeTab === "semantic" ? `project-${id}-sr` : null,
   100→    () => srApi.list(id),
   101→  );
   102→  const { data: runsData, mutate: mutateRuns } = useSWR(
   103→    activeTab === "agent" ? `project-${id}-runs` : null,
   104→    () => agentApi.listRuns(id),
   105→  );
   106→
   107→  /* Handlers */
   108→  const handleDelete = async () => {
   109→    if (!confirm("Delete this project? This will cascade to sources and subscriptions.")) return;
   110→    await projectsApi.delete(id);
   111→    router.push("/projects");
   112→  };
   113→
   114→  const handleTriggerRun = async () => {
   115→    setTriggering(true);
   116→    try {
   117→      await agentApi.triggerRun(id);
   118→      mutateRuns();
   119→    } finally {
   120→      setTriggering(false);
   121→    }
   122→  };
   123→
   124→  const handleDeleteSource = async (sourceId: string) => {
   125→    if (!confirm("Delete this source?")) return;
   126→    await sourcesApi.delete(sourceId);
   127→    mutateSources();
   128→  };
   129→
   130→  const handleDeleteCtx = async (ctxId: string) => {
   131→    if (!confirm("Delete this context source?")) return;
   132→    await ctxApi.delete(ctxId);
   133→    mutateCtx();
   134→  };
   135→
   136→  const handleSaveAgentConfig = async () => {
   137→    if (!project) return;
   138→    setSaving(true);
   139→    try {
   140→      const input = {
   141→        name: project.name,
   142→        description: project.description,
   143→        agent_prompt: promptDraft ?? project.agent_prompt,
   144→        agent_rules: rulesDraft ?? project.agent_rules,
   145→      };
   146→      await projectsApi.update(id, input);
   147→      mutateProject();
   148→      setPromptDraft(null);
   149→      setRulesDraft(null);
   150→    } finally {
   151→      setSaving(false);
   152→    }
   153→  };
   154→
   155→  /* Loading / not found states */
   156→  if (isLoading) {
   157→    return (
   158→      <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   159→        Loading...
   160→      </div>
   161→    );
   162→  }
   163→
   164→  const project = data?.data;
   165→  if (!project) {
   166→    return (
   167→      <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>
   168→        Project not found
   169→      </div>
   170→    );
   171→  }
   172→
   173→  /* Derived counts */
   174→  const sourceCount = sourcesData?.data?.length;
   175→  const ctxCount = ctxData?.data?.length;
   176→
   177→  /* Current effective values */
   178→  const currentPrompt = promptDraft ?? project.agent_prompt ?? "";
   179→  const currentRules: AgentRules = rulesDraft ?? project.agent_rules ?? {};
   180→
   181→  return (
   182→    <div className="space-y-0">
   183→      {/* ===== Header zone ===== */}
   184→      <div
   185→        className="-m-6 mb-0 border-b px-6 py-5"
   186→        style={{ backgroundColor: "#ffffff", borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   187→      >
   188→        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
   189→          {/* Left: project info */}
   190→          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
   191→            <h1
   192→              className="text-[28px] font-bold leading-tight"
   193→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces), serif" }}
   194→            >
   195→              {project.name}
   196→            </h1>
   197→            {project.description && (
   198→              <p
   199→                className="mt-1 text-[14px]"
   200→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif", color: "#6b7280" }}
   201→              >
   202→                {project.description}
   203→              </p>
   204→            )}
   205→            <p
   206→              className="mt-2 text-[12px]"
   207→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   208→            >
   209→              {sourceCount !== undefined ? `${sourceCount} sources` : "-- sources"}
   210→              {" \u00B7 "}
   211→              {ctxCount !== undefined ? `${ctxCount} context sources` : "-- context sources"}
   212→            </p>
   213→          </div>
   214→
   215→          {/* Right: actions */}
   216→          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 ml-4">
   217→            <Link href={`/projects/${id}/edit`}>
   218→              <button
   219→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   220→                style={{
   221→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   222→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   223→                  color: "#374151",
   224→                }}
   225→              >
   226→                <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   227→                Edit
   228→              </button>
   229→            </Link>
   230→            <button
   231→              onClick={handleDelete}
   232→              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-red-50"
   233→              style={{
   234→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   235→                borderColor: "#dc2626",
   236→                color: "#dc2626",
   237→              }}
   238→            >
   239→              <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   240→              Delete
   241→            </button>
   242→            <button
   243→              onClick={handleTriggerRun}
   244→              disabled={triggering}
   245→              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-60"
   246→              style={{
   247→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   248→                backgroundColor: "#e8601a",
   249→              }}
   250→            >
   251→              <Play className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   252→              {triggering ? "Running..." : "Run Agent"}
   253→            </button>
   254→          </div>
   255→        </div>
   256→      </div>
   257→
   258→      {/* ===== Tab bar ===== */}
   259→      <div
   260→        className="-mx-6 border-b px-6"
   261→        style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   262→      >
   263→        <div className="flex gap-0">
   264→          {tabs.map((tab) => {
   265→            const isActive = activeTab === tab.key;
   266→            return (
   267→              <button
   268→                key={tab.key}
   269→                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.key)}
   270→                className="relative px-4 py-2.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors"
   271→                style={{
   272→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   273→                  color: isActive ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   274→                }}
   275→              >
   276→                {tab.label}
   277→                {isActive && (
   278→                  <span
   279→                    className="absolute bottom-0 left-4 right-4 h-[2px]"
   280→                    style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
   281→                  />
   282→                )}
   283→              </button>
   284→            );
   285→          })}
   286→        </div>
   287→      </div>
   288→
   289→      {/* ===== Tab content ===== */}
   290→      <div className="pt-6">
   291→        {/* --- Sources tab --- */}
   292→        {activeTab === "sources" && (
   293→          <div>
   294→            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
   295→              <SectionLabel>Sources</SectionLabel>
   296→              <Link href={`/projects/${id}/sources/new`}>
   297→                <button
   298→                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   299→                  style={{
   300→                    fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   301→                    borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   302→                    color: "#374151",
   303→                  }}
   304→                >
   305→                  <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   306→                  Add Source
   307→                </button>
   308→              </Link>
   309→            </div>
   310→
   311→            {sourcesData?.data && sourcesData.data.length > 0 ? (
   312→              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   313→                <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>
   314→                  <thead>
   315→                    <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   316→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Provider</th>
   317→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Repository</th>
   318→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Interval</th>
   319→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Status</th>
   320→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Last polled</th>
   321→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}></th>
   322→                    </tr>
   323→                  </thead>
   324→                  <tbody>
   325→                    {sourcesData.data.map((source) => (
   326→                      <tr key={source.id} className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   327→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   328→                          <ProviderBadge provider={source.provider} />
   329→                        </td>
   330→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   331→                          <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: "12px" }}>
   332→                            {source.repository}
   333→                          </span>
   334→                        </td>
   335→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>
   336→                          {formatInterval(source.poll_interval_seconds)}
   337→                        </td>
   338→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   339→                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   340→                            <StatusDot status={source.enabled ? "completed" : "pending"} />
   341→                            <span style={{ color: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d97706" }}>
   342→                              {source.enabled ? "Active" : "Disabled"}
   343→                            </span>
   344→                          </div>
   345→                        </td>
   346→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   347→                          {source.last_polled_at
   348→                            ? new Date(source.last_polled_at).toLocaleString()
   349→                            : "Never"}
   350→                        </td>
   351→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
   352→                          <button
   353→                            onClick={() => handleDeleteSource(source.id)}
   354→                            className="text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors hover:text-[#dc2626]"
   355→                            style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   356→                          >
   357→                            Delete
   358→                          </button>
   359→                        </td>
   360→                      </tr>
   361→                    ))}
   362→                  </tbody>
   363→                </table>
   364→              </div>
   365→            ) : (
   366→              <div
   367→                className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   368→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   369→              >
   370→                No sources configured
   371→              </div>
   372→            )}
   373→          </div>
   374→        )}
   375→
   376→        {/* --- Context Sources tab --- */}
   377→        {activeTab === "context" && (
   378→          <div>
   379→            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
   380→              <SectionLabel>Context Sources</SectionLabel>
   381→              <Link href={`/projects/${id}/context-sources/new`}>
   382→                <button
   383→                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   384→                  style={{
   385→                    fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   386→                    borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   387→                    color: "#374151",
   388→                  }}
   389→                >
   390→                  <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   391→                  Add Context Source
   392→                </button>
   393→              </Link>
   394→            </div>
   395→
   396→            {ctxData?.data && ctxData.data.length > 0 ? (
   397→              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   398→                <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>
   399→                  <thead>
   400→                    <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   401→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Type</th>
   402→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Name</th>
   403→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Config URL</th>
   404→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}></th>
   405→                    </tr>
   406→                  </thead>
   407→                  <tbody>
   408→                    {ctxData.data.map((ctx) => (
   409→                      <tr key={ctx.id} className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   410→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   411→                          <span
   412→                            className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none"
   413→                            style={{ backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1", color: "#374151" }}
   414→                          >
   415→                            {ctx.type}
   416→                          </span>
   417→                        </td>
   418→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium" style={{ color: "#111113" }}>
   419→                          {ctx.name}
   420→                        </td>
   421→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   422→                          <span
   423→                            className="text-[12px]"
   424→                            style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
   425→                            title={typeof ctx.config?.url === "string" ? ctx.config.url : ""}
   426→                          >
   427→                            {typeof ctx.config?.url === "string"
   428→                              ? truncate(ctx.config.url, 50)
   429→                              : "--"}
   430→                          </span>
   431→                        </td>
   432→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
   433→                          <button
   434→                            onClick={() => handleDeleteCtx(ctx.id)}
   435→                            className="text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors hover:text-[#dc2626]"
   436→                            style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   437→                          >
   438→                            Delete
   439→                          </button>
   440→                        </td>
   441→                      </tr>
   442→                    ))}
   443→                  </tbody>
   444→                </table>
   445→              </div>
   446→            ) : (
   447→              <div
   448→                className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   449→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   450→              >
   451→                No context sources configured
   452→              </div>
   453→            )}
   454→          </div>
   455→        )}
   456→
   457→        {/* --- Semantic Releases tab --- */}
   458→        {activeTab === "semantic" && (
   459→          <div>
   460→            <SectionLabel className="mb-4">Semantic Releases</SectionLabel>
   461→
   462→            {srData?.data && srData.data.length > 0 ? (
   463→              <div className="space-y-3">
   464→                {srData.data.map((sr) => (
   465→                  <Link
   466→                    key={sr.id}
   467→                    href={`/projects/${id}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}
   468→                    className="block rounded-md border p-4 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   469→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   470→                  >
   471→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
   472→                      <VersionChip version={sr.version} />
   473→                      <StatusDot status={sr.status} />
   474→                      <span className="text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>
   475→                        {sr.status}
   476→                      </span>
   477→                      {sr.report?.urgency && <UrgencyChip urgency={sr.report.urgency} />}
   478→                    </div>
   479→                    {sr.report?.summary && (
   480→                      <p
   481→                        className="mt-2 text-[13px] italic leading-relaxed"
   482→                        style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}
   483→                      >
   484→                        {truncate(sr.report.summary, 200)}
   485→                      </p>
   486→                    )}
   487→                  </Link>
   488→                ))}
   489→              </div>
   490→            ) : (
   491→              <div
   492→                className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   493→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   494→              >
   495→                No semantic releases yet
   496→              </div>
   497→            )}
   498→          </div>
   499→        )}
   500→
   501→        {/* --- Agent tab --- */}
   502→        {activeTab === "agent" && (
   503→          <div className="space-y-8">
   504→            {/* Prompt section */}
   505→            <div>
   506→              <SectionLabel className="mb-3">Agent Prompt</SectionLabel>
   507→              <textarea
   508→                value={currentPrompt}
   509→                onChange={(e) => setPromptDraft(e.target.value)}
   510→                rows={5}
   511→                placeholder="Custom instructions for the agent when analyzing releases..."
   512→                className="w-full resize-y rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-[13px] placeholder:text-[#9ca3af] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   513→                style={{
   514→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   515→                  backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   516→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   517→                  color: "#111113",
   518→                }}
   519→              />
   520→              <div className="mt-2 flex justify-end">
   521→                <button
   522→                  onClick={handleSaveAgentConfig}
   523→                  disabled={saving || (promptDraft === null && rulesDraft === null)}
   524→                  className="rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   525→                  style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
   526→                >
   527→                  {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}
   528→                </button>
   529→              </div>
   530→            </div>
   531→
   532→            {/* Rules section */}
   533→            <div>
   534→              <SectionLabel className="mb-3">Trigger Rules</SectionLabel>
   535→              <div className="space-y-3">
   536→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   537→                  <input
   538→                    type="checkbox"
   539→                    checked={currentRules.on_major_release ?? false}
   540→                    onChange={(e) =>
   541→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_major_release: e.target.checked })
   542→                    }
   543→                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border accent-[#e8601a]"
   544→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   545→                  />
   546→                  On Major Release
   547→                </label>
   548→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   549→                  <input
   550→                    type="checkbox"
   551→                    checked={currentRules.on_minor_release ?? false}
   552→                    onChange={(e) =>
   553→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_minor_release: e.target.checked })
   554→                    }
   555→                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border accent-[#e8601a]"
   556→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   557→                  />
   558→                  On Minor Release
   559→                </label>
   560→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   561→                  <input
   562→                    type="checkbox"
   563→                    checked={currentRules.on_security_patch ?? false}
   564→                    onChange={(e) =>
   565→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_security_patch: e.target.checked })
   566→                    }
   567→                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border accent-[#e8601a]"
   568→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   569→                  />
   570→                  On Security Patch
   571→                </label>
   572→                <div className="mt-4">
   573→                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   574→                    Version Pattern
   575→                  </label>
   576→                  <input
   577→                    type="text"
   578→                    value={currentRules.version_pattern ?? ""}
   579→                    onChange={(e) =>
   580→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, version_pattern: e.target.value })
   581→                    }
   582→                    placeholder="e.g. ^v\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+$"
   583→                    className="w-full max-w-md rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] placeholder:text-[#9ca3af] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   584→                    style={{
   585→                      fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   586→                      backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   587→                      borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   588→                      color: "#111113",
   589→                    }}
   590→                  />
   591→                </div>
   592→              </div>
   593→            </div>
   594→
   595→            {/* Run History section */}
   596→            <div>
   597→              <SectionLabel className="mb-3">Run History</SectionLabel>
   598→
   599→              {runsData?.data && runsData.data.length > 0 ? (
   600→                <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   601→                  <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>
   602→                    <thead>
   603→                      <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   604→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Trigger</th>
   605→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Status</th>
   606→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Started</th>
   607→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Duration</th>
   608→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Semantic Release</th>
   609→                      </tr>
   610→                    </thead>
   611→                    <tbody>
   612→                      {runsData.data.map((run) => (
   613→                        <tr key={run.id} className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   614→                          <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   615→                            {run.trigger}
   616→                          </td>
   617→                          <td className="px-4 py-3">
   618→                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   619→                              <StatusDot status={run.status} />
   620→                              <span style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>{run.status}</span>
   621→                            </div>
   622→                          </td>
   623→                          <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   624→                            {run.started_at
   625→                              ? new Date(run.started_at).toLocaleString()
   626→                              : "Pending"}
   627→                          </td>
   628→                          <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   629→                            {formatDuration(run.started_at, run.completed_at)}
   630→                          </td>
   631→                          <td className="px-4 py-3">
   632→                            {run.semantic_release_id ? (
   633→                              <Link
   634→                                href={`/projects/${id}/semantic-releases/${run.semantic_release_id}`}
   635→                                className="text-[13px] font-medium underline"
   636→                                style={{ color: "#e8601a" }}
   637→                              >
   638→                                View
   639→                              </Link>
   640→                            ) : (
   641→                              <span style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>--</span>
   642→                            )}
   643→                          </td>
   644→                        </tr>
   645→                      ))}
   646→                    </tbody>
   647→                  </table>
   648→                </div>
   649→              ) : (
   650→                <div
   651→                  className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   652→                  style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   653→                >
   654→                  No agent runs yet
   655→                </div>
   656→              )}
   657→            </div>
   658→          </div>
   659→        )}
   660→      </div>
   661→    </div>
   662→  );
   663→}

     1→# Projects Page & Onboarding Redesign — Implementation Plan
     2→
     3→> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.
     4→
     5→**Goal:** Replace the projects table with dashboard cards showing sources/releases inline, simplify project creation to include optional source fields instead of agent config, and add inline source creation on the projects page.
     6→
     7→**Architecture:** Frontend-only changes. No backend modifications. Three files rewritten (`projects/page.tsx`, `project-form.tsx`), one minor edit (`project-edit.tsx`), one new page file unchanged but with different `onSubmit` logic.
     8→
     9→**Tech Stack:** Next.js, React, SWR, Tailwind CSS, Lucide icons, react-icons (FaGithub, FaDocker)
    10→

    12→
    13→### Task 1: Simplify ProjectForm — Remove Agent Fields, Add Source Fields
    14→
    15→**Files:**
    16→- Modify: `web/components/projects/project-form.tsx` (full rewrite)
    17→
    18→**Step 1: Rewrite ProjectForm**
    19→
    20→Replace the entire `web/components/projects/project-form.tsx` with a form that has:
    21→- Name (required text input)
    22→- Description (optional textarea)
    23→- Optional "Add a Source" section with: provider dropdown, repository input, poll interval input
    24→
    25→The form's `onSubmit` callback now returns `{ project: ProjectInput, source?: SourceInput }` instead of just `ProjectInput`.
    26→
    27→```tsx
    28→"use client";
    29→
    30→import { useState } from "react";
    31→import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
    32→import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
    33→import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
    34→import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
    35→import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
    36→import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
    37→import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
    38→import type { Project, ProjectInput, SourceInput } from "@/lib/api/types";
    39→import { Plus, X } from "lucide-react";
    40→
    41→export interface ProjectFormResult {
    42→  project: ProjectInput;
    43→  source?: SourceInput;
    44→}
    45→
    46→interface ProjectFormProps {
    47→  initial?: Project;
    48→  onSubmit: (result: ProjectFormResult) => Promise<void>;
    49→  title: string;
    50→  /** Hide the source section (used in edit mode) */
    51→  hideSource?: boolean;
    52→}
    53→
    54→export function ProjectForm({ initial, onSubmit, title, hideSource }: ProjectFormProps) {
    55→  const router = useRouter();
    56→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    57→  const [name, setName] = useState(initial?.name ?? "");
    58→  const [description, setDescription] = useState(initial?.description ?? "");
    59→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    60→
    61→  /* Source fields (only for create) */
    62→  const [showSource, setShowSource] = useState(false);
    63→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("github");
    64→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState("");
    65→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState("300");
    66→
    67→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    68→    e.preventDefault();
    69→    setError("");
    70→    setSaving(true);
    71→    try {
    72→      const result: ProjectFormResult = {
    73→        project: {
    74→          name,
    75→          description: description || undefined,
    76→        },
    77→      };
    78→      if (showSource && repository.trim()) {
    79→        result.source = {
    80→          provider,
    81→          repository: repository.trim(),
    82→          poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval) || 300,
    83→          enabled: true,
    84→        };
    85→      }
    86→      await onSubmit(result);
    87→      router.push("/projects");
    88→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    89→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save");
    90→    } finally {
    91→      setSaving(false);
    92→    }
    93→  };
    94→
    95→  return (
    96→    <Card className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
    97→      <CardHeader>
    98→        <CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle>
    99→      </CardHeader>
   100→      <CardContent>
   101→        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
   102→          {error && <div className="rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>}
   103→          <div className="space-y-2">
   104→            <Label htmlFor="name">Name</Label>
   105→            <Input id="name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} required />
   106→          </div>
   107→          <div className="space-y-2">
   108→            <Label htmlFor="description">Description</Label>
   109→            <Textarea id="description" value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} rows={2} />
   110→          </div>
   111→
   112→          {/* Optional source section — only in create mode */}
   113→          {!hideSource && (
   114→            <div className="space-y-3">
   115→              {!showSource ? (
   116→                <button
   117→                  type="button"
   118→                  onClick={() => setShowSource(true)}
   119→                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:opacity-80"
   120→                  style={{ color: "#e8601a" }}
   121→                >
   122→                  <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   123→                  Add a Source
   124→                </button>
   125→              ) : (
   126→                <div className="rounded-md border p-4 space-y-3" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   127→                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   128→                    <Label className="text-[13px] font-medium">Add a Source</Label>
   129→                    <button
   130→                      type="button"
   131→                      onClick={() => { setShowSource(false); setRepository(""); }}
   132→                      className="text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
   133→                    >
   134→                      <X className="h-4 w-4" />
   135→                    </button>
   136→                  </div>
   137→                  <div className="space-y-2">
   138→                    <Label>Provider</Label>
   139→                    <Select value={provider} onValueChange={setProvider}>
   140→                      <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
   141→                      <SelectContent>
   142→                        <SelectItem value="github">GitHub</SelectItem>
   143→                        <SelectItem value="dockerhub">Docker Hub</SelectItem>
   144→                      </SelectContent>
   145→                    </Select>
   146→                  </div>
   147→                  <div className="space-y-2">
   148→                    <Label htmlFor="repository">Repository</Label>
   149→                    <Input
   150→                      id="repository"
   151→                      value={repository}
   152→                      onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)}
   153→                      placeholder="e.g. ethereum/go-ethereum"
   154→                    />
   155→                  </div>
   156→                  <div className="space-y-2">
   157→                    <Label htmlFor="poll_interval">Poll Interval (seconds)</Label>
   158→                    <Input
   159→                      id="poll_interval"
   160→                      type="number"
   161→                      min={60}
   162→                      value={pollInterval}
   163→                      onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)}
   164→                    />
   165→                  </div>
   166→                </div>
   167→              )}
   168→            </div>
   169→          )}
   170→
   171→          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
   172→            <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.back()}>Cancel</Button>
   173→            <Button type="submit" disabled={saving}>{saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</Button>
   174→          </div>
   175→        </form>
   176→      </CardContent>
   177→    </Card>
   178→  );
   179→}
   180→```

   250→
   251→```tsx
   252→"use client";
   253→
   254→import useSWR from "swr";
   255→import { projects as projectsApi } from "@/lib/api/client";
   256→import { ProjectForm } from "@/components/projects/project-form";
   257→
   258→export function ProjectEdit({ id }: { id: string }) {
   259→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(`project-${id}`, () => projectsApi.get(id));
   260→
   261→  if (isLoading) return <div className="py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground">Loading...</div>;
   262→  if (!data?.data) return <div className="py-12 text-center">Project not found</div>;
   263→
   264→  return (
   265→    <ProjectForm
   266→      title="Edit Project"
   267→      initial={data.data}
   268→      hideSource
   269→      onSubmit={async (result) => { await projectsApi.update(id, result.project); }}
   270→    />
   271→  );
   272→}

   289→### Task 4: Rewrite Projects Page — Dashboard Cards with Inline Source Add
   290→
   291→**Files:**
