   293→
   294→This is the largest task. Replace the table layout with dashboard-style project cards.
   295→
   296→**Step 1: Rewrite the projects page**
   297→
   298→The new page renders each project as a card with three sections:
   299→
   300→1. **Header** — project name (link to detail), description, edit link
   301→2. **Sources** — compact list of sources with provider icon, repo, status dot, poll interval, and inline "Add Source" form
   302→3. **Recent Releases** — latest 5 releases across all sources with version chip, provider icon, repo name, relative time
   303→
   304→```tsx
   305→"use client";
   306→
   307→import { useState } from "react";
   308→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
   309→import Link from "next/link";
   310→import {
   311→  projects as projectsApi,
   312→  releases as releasesApi,
   313→  sources as sourcesApi,
   314→} from "@/lib/api/client";
   315→import { getProviderIcon } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
   316→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
   317→import { Plus, X, Pencil } from "lucide-react";
   318→import type { Project, Source, Release } from "@/lib/api/types";
   319→
   320→/* ---------- Inline Add Source Form ---------- */
   321→
   322→function InlineSourceForm({
   323→  projectId,
   324→  onDone,
   325→}: {
   326→  projectId: string;
   327→  onDone: () => void;
   328→}) {
   329→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("github");
   330→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState("");
   331→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState("300");
   332→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   333→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
   334→
   335→  const handleAdd = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   336→    e.preventDefault();
   337→    if (!repository.trim()) return;
   338→    setSaving(true);
   339→    setError("");
   340→    try {
   341→      await sourcesApi.create(projectId, {
   342→        provider,
   343→        repository: repository.trim(),
   344→        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval) || 300,
   345→        enabled: true,
   346→      });
   347→      // Revalidate sources for this project
   348→      mutate(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`);
   349→      onDone();
   350→    } catch (err: unknown) {
   351→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to add source");
   352→    } finally {
   353→      setSaving(false);
   354→    }
   355→  };
   356→
   357→  return (
   358→    <form onSubmit={handleAdd} className="mt-2 rounded-md border p-3 space-y-2" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   359→      {error && <div className="text-[12px] text-red-600">{error}</div>}
   360→      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   361→        <select
   362→          value={provider}
   363→          onChange={(e) => setProvider(e.target.value)}
   364→          className="rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
   365→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   366→        >
   367→          <option value="github">GitHub</option>
   368→          <option value="dockerhub">Docker Hub</option>
   369→        </select>
   370→        <input
   371→          type="text"
   372→          value={repository}
   373→          onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)}
   374→          placeholder="e.g. ethereum/go-ethereum"
   375→          className="flex-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
   376→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
   377→        />
   378→        <input
   379→          type="number"
   380→          min={60}
   381→          value={pollInterval}
   382→          onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)}
   383→          className="w-20 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
   384→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   385→          title="Poll interval (seconds)"
   386→        />
   387→      </div>
   388→      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
   389→        <button
   390→          type="button"
   391→          onClick={onDone}
   392→          className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
   393→        >
   394→          Cancel
   395→        </button>
   396→        <button
   397→          type="submit"
   398→          disabled={saving || !repository.trim()}
   399→          className="rounded-md px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40"
   400→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
   401→        >
   402→          {saving ? "Adding..." : "Add"}
   403→        </button>
   404→      </div>
   405→    </form>
   406→  );
   407→}
   408→
   409→/* ---------- Sources Section ---------- */
   410→
   411→function SourcesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   412→  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
   413→  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
   414→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
   415→  );

   417→
   418→  return (
   419→    <div
   420→      className="border-t px-4 py-3"
   421→      style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   422→    >
   423→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
   424→        <span
   425→          className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]"
   426→          style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   427→        >
   428→          Sources
   429→        </span>
   430→        {!adding && (
   431→          <button
   432→            onClick={() => setAdding(true)}
   433→            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors hover:opacity-80"
   434→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   435→          >
   436→            <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
   437→            Add Source
   438→          </button>
   439→        )}
   440→        {adding && (
   441→          <button
   442→            onClick={() => setAdding(false)}
   443→            className="text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
   444→          >
   445→            <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   446→          </button>
   447→        )}
   448→      </div>
   449→
   450→      {sources.length > 0 ? (
   451→        <div className="space-y-1">
   452→          {sources.map((source) => {
   453→            const Icon = getProviderIcon(source.provider);
   454→            return (
   455→              <div
   456→                key={source.id}
   457→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px]"
   458→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   459→              >
   460→                {Icon && <Icon size={13} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />}
   461→                <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: "#374151" }}>
   462→                  {source.repository}
   463→                </span>
   464→                <span
   465→                  className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]"
   466→                  style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   467→                >
   468→                  <span
   469→                    className="inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full"
   470→                    style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   471→                  />
   472→                  {source.enabled ? "Active" : "Disabled"}
   473→                  <span className="ml-1" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   474→                    {source.poll_interval_seconds < 60
   475→                      ? `${source.poll_interval_seconds}s`
   476→                      : source.poll_interval_seconds < 3600
   477→                        ? `${Math.round(source.poll_interval_seconds / 60)}m`
   478→                        : `${(source.poll_interval_seconds / 3600).toFixed(1)}h`}
   479→                  </span>
   480→                </span>
   481→              </div>
   482→            );
   483→          })}
   484→        </div>
   485→      ) : (
   486→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   487→          No sources configured
   488→        </p>
   489→      )}
   490→
   491→      {adding && (
   492→        <InlineSourceForm projectId={projectId} onDone={() => setAdding(false)} />
   493→      )}
   494→    </div>
   495→  );
   496→}
   497→
   498→/* ---------- Recent Releases Section ---------- */
   499→
   500→function RecentReleasesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   501→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-releases`, () =>
   502→    releasesApi.listByProject(projectId, 1, 5)
   503→  );
   504→  const { data: srcData } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
   505→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
   506→  );
   507→  const releases = relData?.data ?? [];
   508→  const sources = srcData?.data ?? [];
   509→
   510→  const sourceMap = new Map<string, Source>();
   511→  for (const s of sources) sourceMap.set(s.id, s);
   512→
   513→  return (
   514→    <div className="border-t px-4 py-3" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   515→      <span
   516→        className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mb-1 block"
   517→        style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   518→      >
   519→        Recent Releases
   520→      </span>
   521→
   522→      {releases.length > 0 ? (
   523→        <div className="space-y-1">
   524→          {releases.map((r) => {
   525→            const src = sourceMap.get(r.source_id);
   526→            const Icon = src ? getProviderIcon(src.provider) : undefined;
   527→            return (
   528→              <Link
   529→                key={r.id}
   530→                href={`/releases/${r.id}`}
   531→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] rounded px-1 -mx-1 py-0.5"
   532→              >
   533→                <span
   534→                  className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none"
   535→                  style={{
   536→                    backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1",
   537→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   538→                    color: "#374151",
   539→                  }}
   540→                >
   541→                  {r.version}
   542→                </span>
   543→                {Icon && <Icon size={11} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} />}
   544→                {src && (
   545→                  <span className="text-[11px] truncate" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   546→                    {src.repository}
   547→                  </span>
   548→                )}
   549→                <span className="ml-auto text-[11px] shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   550→                  {timeAgo(r.released_at || r.created_at)}
   551→                </span>
   552→              </Link>
   553→            );
   554→          })}
   555→          <Link
   556→            href={`/projects/${projectId}`}
   557→            className="block text-[11px] mt-1 hover:underline"
   558→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   559→          >
   560→            View all &rarr;
   561→          </Link>
   562→        </div>
   563→      ) : (
   564→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   565→          No releases yet
   566→        </p>
   567→      )}
   568→    </div>
   569→  );
   570→}
   571→
   572→/* ---------- Project Card ---------- */
   573→
   574→function ProjectCard({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   575→  return (
   576→    <div
   577→      className="overflow-hidden rounded-md"
   578→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   579→    >
   580→      {/* Header */}
   581→      <div className="flex items-start justify-between px-4 py-3">
   582→        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
   583→          <Link
   584→            href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
   585→            className="text-[16px] font-bold hover:underline"
   586→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#111113" }}
   587→          >
   588→            {project.name}
   589→          </Link>
   590→          {project.description && (
   591→            <p
   592→              className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] truncate"
   593→              style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   594→            >
   595→              {project.description}
   596→            </p>
   597→          )}
   598→        </div>
   599→        <Link
   600→          href={`/projects/${project.id}/edit`}
   601→          className="ml-2 shrink-0 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   602→          style={{
   603→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   604→            borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   605→            color: "#6b7280",
   606→          }}
   607→        >
   608→          <Pencil className="h-3 w-3" />
   609→          Edit
   610→        </Link>
   611→      </div>
   612→
   613→      {/* Sources */}
   614→      <SourcesSection projectId={project.id} />
   615→
   616→      {/* Recent Releases */}
   617→      <RecentReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
   618→    </div>
   619→  );
   620→}
   621→
   622→/* ---------- Page ---------- */
   623→
   624→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   625→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list());
   626→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   627→
   628→  return (
   629→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   630→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   631→        <div>
   632→          <h1
   633→            style={{
   634→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   635→              fontSize: "24px",
   636→              fontWeight: 700,
   637→              color: "#111113",
   638→            }}
   639→          >
   640→            Projects
   641→          </h1>
   642→          <p
   643→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   644→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   645→          >
   646→            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.
   647→          </p>
   648→        </div>
   649→        <Link
   650→          href="/projects/new"
   651→          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   652→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   653→        >
   654→          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   655→          New Project
   656→        </Link>
   657→      </div>
   658→
   659→      {isLoading ? (
   660→        <p
   661→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   662→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   663→        >
   664→          Loading...
   665→        </p>
   666→      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   667→        <div
   668→          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-md border py-12"
   669→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   670→        >
   671→          <p
   672→            className="text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   673→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   674→          >
   675→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   676→          </p>
   677→          <Link
   678→            href="/projects/new"
   679→            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   680→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   681→          >
   682→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   683→            New Project
   684→          </Link>
   685→        </div>
   686→      ) : (
   687→        <div className="space-y-4">
   688→          {items.map((project) => (
   689→            <ProjectCard key={project.id} project={project} />
   690→          ))}
   691→        </div>
   692→      )}
   693→    </div>
   694→  );
   695→}
   696→```

The task is complete. Here is a summary of what was done:

**File modified:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/projects/page.tsx`

**What changed:**

The projects page was rewritten from a table-based layout to a dashboard-style card layout. The key changes are:

1. **Table replaced with cards** -- The old `<table>` with columns (Name, Description, Recent Releases, Semantic Releases, Created) was removed. Each project now renders as a `ProjectCard` component with a header, sources section, and recent releases section stacked vertically.

2. **New `InlineSourceForm` component** -- An inline form that lets users add a new source (GitHub or Docker Hub) directly from within a project card, with fields for provider, repository, and poll interval. Uses `mutate()` from SWR to revalidate the sources list after adding.

3. **New `SourcesSection` component** -- Displays each project's configured sources with provider icon, repository name, active/disabled status indicator (green/gray dot), and formatted poll interval. Includes an "Add Source" toggle button.

4. **New `RecentReleasesSection` component** -- Shows the 5 most recent releases per project with version badges, provider icons, repository names, and relative timestamps. Includes a "View all" link to the project detail page.

5. **New `ProjectCard` component** -- Composes the header (project name + edit button), sources section, and recent releases section into a bordered card.

6. **Empty state improved** -- The empty state now includes a centered "New Project" call-to-action button.

7. **Removed dependencies** -- The `semanticReleases` API import and the `SemanticReleaseChips`/`ReleaseChips`/`ProjectRow` components were removed. Added new imports: `mutate` from SWR, `X` and `Pencil` from lucide-react, and `Source`/`Release` types.

**Commit:** `935e8bc` on branch `main`.

> AGENT

All tasks complete. Here's the summary:

**Projects page redesign — done.** Three changes shipped across 5 commits:

1. **ProjectForm simplified** — Removed agent prompt and trigger rules fields. Added optional "Add a Source" section with provider/repository/poll interval. Edit mode hides the source section via `hideSource` prop.

2. **New project creation** — Creates the project first, then creates the source if one was provided. Two sequential API calls, clean error handling.

3. **Projects page dashboard cards** — Replaced the flat table with stacked cards per project. Each card has:
   - Header with project name (links to detail) and edit button
   - Sources section showing provider icon, repository, active/disabled status, poll interval, and inline "Add Source" form
   - Recent Releases section showing latest 5 releases with version chips, provider icons, repo names, and relative timestamps

Build passes cleanly. No backend changes needed — all existing API endpoints reused as-is.

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import useSWR from "swr";
     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import {
     7→  releases as releasesApi,
     8→  projects as projectsApi,
     9→  sources as sourcesApi,
    10→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    11→import { ProviderBadge } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    12→import { VersionChip } from "@/components/ui/version-chip";
    13→import type { Release, Source, Project } from "@/lib/api/types";
    14→import { ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
    15→
    16→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    17→
    18→interface ReleaseRow extends Release {
    19→  _projectName?: string;
    20→  _projectId?: string;
    21→  _provider?: string;
    22→  _repository?: string;
    23→}
    24→
    25→const PER_PAGE = 15;
    26→
    27→function getProviderUrl(
    28→  provider: string,
    29→  repository: string,
    30→  version: string
    31→): string | null {
    32→  switch (provider) {
    33→    case "github":
    34→      return `https://github.com/${repository}/releases/tag/${version}`;
    35→    case "dockerhub":
    36→      return `https://hub.docker.com/r/${repository}/tags?name=${encodeURIComponent(version)}`;
    37→    default:
    38→      return null;
    39→  }
    40→}
    41→
    42→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    43→/*  Page                                                               */
    44→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    45→
    46→export default function ReleasesPage() {
    47→  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    48→  const [projectFilter, setProjectFilter] = useState<string>("all");
    49→
    50→  /* Fetch projects for the filter dropdown + source enrichment */
    51→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-filter", () =>
    52→    projectsApi.list()
    53→  );
    54→
    55→  /* Build a map: sourceId -> { provider, repository, projectName, projectId } */
    56→  const { data: sourceMap } = useSWR(
    57→    projectsData ? "source-map-for-releases" : null,
    58→    async () => {
    59→      if (!projectsData?.data?.length) return new Map<string, { provider: string; repository: string; projectName: string; projectId: string }>();
    60→      const map = new Map<string, { provider: string; repository: string; projectName: string; projectId: string }>();
    61→      await Promise.all(
    62→        projectsData.data.map(async (p: Project) => {
    63→          const res = await sourcesApi.listByProject(p.id).catch(() => null);
    64→          if (res?.data) {
    65→            for (const s of res.data) {
    66→              map.set(s.id, {
    67→                provider: s.provider,
    68→                repository: s.repository,
    69→                projectName: p.name,
    70→                projectId: p.id,
    71→              });
    72→            }
    73→          }
    74→        })
    75→      );
    76→      return map;
    77→    }
    78→  );
    79→
    80→  /* Fetch releases — scoped by project or all */
    81→  const { data: scopedData, isLoading: scopedLoading } = useSWR(
    82→    projectFilter !== "all" ? ["releases", page, projectFilter] : null,
    83→    () => releasesApi.listByProject(projectFilter, page, PER_PAGE)
    84→  );
    85→
    86→  const { data: allReleasesData, isLoading: allLoading } = useSWR(
    87→    projectFilter === "all" ? ["all-releases", page] : null,
    88→    () => releasesApi.list(page, PER_PAGE)
    89→  );
    90→
    91→  const isLoading = projectFilter !== "all" ? scopedLoading : allLoading;
    92→
    93→  /* Enrich releases with source metadata */
    94→  const rawReleases: Release[] =
    95→    projectFilter !== "all"
    96→      ? scopedData?.data ?? []
    97→      : allReleasesData?.data ?? [];
    98→
    99→  const releases: ReleaseRow[] = rawReleases.map((r) => {
   100→    const info = sourceMap?.get(r.source_id);
   101→    return {
   102→      ...r,
   103→      _projectName: info?.projectName,
   104→      _projectId: info?.projectId,
   105→      _provider: info?.provider,
   106→      _repository: info?.repository,
   107→    };
   108→  });
   109→
   110→  /* Pagination math */
   111→  const activeData = projectFilter !== "all" ? scopedData : allReleasesData;
   112→  const total = activeData?.meta?.total ?? 0;
   113→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PER_PAGE));
   114→  const startRow = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE + 1;
   115→  const endRow = Math.min(page * PER_PAGE, total);
   116→
   117→  return (
   118→    <div className="space-y-6">
   119→      {/* Page title */}
   120→      <h1
   121→        style={{
   122→          fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   123→          fontSize: "24px",
   124→          fontWeight: 700,
   125→          color: "#111113",
   126→        }}
   127→      >
   128→        Releases
   129→      </h1>
   130→
   131→      {/* Project filter */}
   132→      <div>
   133→        <select
   134→          value={projectFilter}
   135→          onChange={(e) => {
   136→            setProjectFilter(e.target.value);
   137→            setPage(1);
   138→          }}
   139→          className="appearance-none rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-2 pr-8 outline-none transition-shadow"
   140→          style={{
   141→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   142→            fontSize: "13px",
   143→            color: "#111113",
   144→            border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   145→          }}
   146→          onFocus={(e) =>
   147→            (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = "0 0 0 2px #e8601a40")
   148→          }
   149→          onBlur={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = "none")}
   150→        >
   151→          <option value="all">All Projects</option>
   152→          {projectsData?.data.map((p: Project) => (
   153→            <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
   154→              {p.name}
   155→            </option>
   156→          ))}
   157→        </select>
   158→      </div>
   159→
   160→      {/* Table card */}
   161→      <div
   162→        className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white"
   163→        style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   164→      >
   165→        {isLoading ? (
   166→          <div
   167→            className="py-16 text-center"
   168→            style={{
   169→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   170→              fontSize: "13px",
   171→              color: "#6b7280",
   172→            }}
   173→          >
   174→            Loading...
   175→          </div>
   176→        ) : releases.length === 0 ? (
   177→          <div className="py-16 text-center">
   178→            <p
   179→              style={{
   180→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   181→                fontStyle: "italic",
   182→                fontSize: "15px",
   183→                color: "#9ca3af",
   184→              }}
   185→            >
   186→              No releases ingested yet
   187→            </p>
   188→          </div>
   189→        ) : (
   190→          <table className="w-full">
   191→            <thead>
   192→              <tr style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   193→                {["Project", "Provider", "Repository", "Version", "Released", "Age", ""].map(
   194→                  (col) => (
   195→                    <th
   196→                      key={col}
   197→                      className="px-4 py-3 text-left"
   198→                      style={{
   199→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   200→                        fontSize: "11px",
   201→                        fontWeight: 600,
   202→                        textTransform: "uppercase" as const,
   203→                        letterSpacing: "0.08em",
   204→                        color: "#9ca3af",
   205→                      }}
   206→                    >
   207→                      {col}
   208→                    </th>
   209→                  )
   210→                )}
   211→              </tr>
   212→            </thead>
   213→            <tbody>
   214→              {releases.map((release) => (
   215→                <tr
   216→                  key={release.id}
   217→                  className="transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   218→                  style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   219→                >
   220→                  {/* Project */}
   221→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   222→                    {release._projectId ? (
   223→                      <Link
   224→                        href={`/projects/${release._projectId}`}
   225→                        className="hover:underline"
   226→                        style={{
   227→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   228→                          fontSize: "13px",
   229→                          color: "#111113",
   230→                          fontWeight: 500,
   231→                        }}
   232→                      >
   233→                        {release._projectName ?? "\u2014"}
   234→                      </Link>
   235→                    ) : (
   236→                      <span
   237→                        style={{
   238→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   239→                          fontSize: "13px",
   240→                          color: "#9ca3af",
   241→                        }}
   242→                      >
   243→                        {"\u2014"}
   244→                      </span>
   245→                    )}
   246→                  </td>
   247→
   248→                  {/* Provider */}
   249→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   250→                    {release._provider ? (
   251→                      <ProviderBadge provider={release._provider} />
   252→                    ) : (
   253→                      <span
   254→                        style={{
   255→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   256→                          fontSize: "13px",
   257→                          color: "#9ca3af",
   258→                        }}
   259→                      >
   260→                        {"\u2014"}
   261→                      </span>
   262→                    )}
   263→                  </td>
   264→
   265→                  {/* Repository */}
   266→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   267→                    <span
   268→                      style={{
   269→                        fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   270→                        fontSize: "12px",
   271→                        color: "#374151",
   272→                      }}
   273→                    >
   274→                      {release._repository ?? release.source_id}
   275→                    </span>
   276→                  </td>
   277→
   278→                  {/* Version */}
   279→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   280→                    <Link href={`/releases/${release.id}`}>
   281→                      <VersionChip version={release.version} />
   282→                    </Link>
   283→                  </td>
   284→
   285→                  {/* Released date */}
   286→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   287→                    <span
   288→                      style={{
   289→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   290→                        fontSize: "13px",
   291→                        color: "#6b7280",
   292→                      }}
   293→                    >
   294→                      {release.released_at
   295→                        ? new Date(release.released_at).toLocaleDateString()
   296→                        : "\u2014"}
   297→                    </span>
   298→                  </td>
   299→
   300→                  {/* Age */}
   301→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   302→                    <span
   303→                      style={{
   304→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   305→                        fontSize: "13px",
   306→                        color: "#9ca3af",
   307→                      }}
   308→                    >
   309→                      {timeAgo(release.released_at ?? release.created_at)}
   310→                    </span>
   311→                  </td>
   312→
   313→                  {/* Provider link */}
   314→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   315→                    {release._provider && release._repository && (() => {
   316→                      const url = getProviderUrl(release._provider, release._repository, release.version);
   317→                      return url ? (
   318→                        <a
   319→                          href={url}
   320→                          target="_blank"
   321→                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
   322→                          className="inline-flex items-center transition-colors hover:opacity-70"
   323→                          style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   324→                          title="View on provider"
   325→                        >
   326→                          <ExternalLink size={14} />
   327→                        </a>
   328→                      ) : null;
   329→                    })()}
   330→                  </td>
   331→                </tr>
   332→              ))}
   333→            </tbody>
   334→          </table>
   335→        )}
   336→      </div>
   337→
   338→      {/* Pagination */}
   339→      {total > 0 && (
   340→        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   341→          <span
   342→            style={{
   343→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   344→              fontSize: "13px",
   345→              color: "#9ca3af",
   346→            }}
   347→          >
   348→            {startRow}&ndash;{endRow} of {total}
   349→          </span>
   350→          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   351→            <button
   352→              disabled={page <= 1}
   353→              onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}
   354→              className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
   355→              style={{
   356→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   357→                fontSize: "13px",
   358→                color: "#374151",
   359→                border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   360→              }}
   361→            >
   362→              Previous
   363→            </button>
   364→            <button
   365→              disabled={page >= totalPages}
   366→              onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}
   367→              className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
   368→              style={{
   369→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   370→                fontSize: "13px",
   371→                color: "#374151",
   372→                border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   373→              }}
   374→            >
   375→              Next
   376→            </button>
   377→          </div>
   378→        </div>
   379→      )}
   380→    </div>
   381→  );
   382→}

     1→package ingestion
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"log/slog"
     7→	"net/http"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// SourceLoader reads source configuration from the database and constructs
    13→// IIngestionSource instances. It bridges the API-managed sources table with
    14→// the polling orchestrator.
    15→type SourceLoader struct {
    16→	pool   *pgxpool.Pool
    17→	client *http.Client
    18→}
    19→
    20→func NewSourceLoader(pool *pgxpool.Pool, client *http.Client) *SourceLoader {
    21→	return &SourceLoader{pool: pool, client: client}
    22→}
    23→
    24→// LoadEnabledSources queries all enabled sources and constructs the
    25→// appropriate IIngestionSource for each one based on provider.
    26→func (l *SourceLoader) LoadEnabledSources(ctx context.Context) ([]IIngestionSource, error) {
    27→	rows, err := l.pool.Query(ctx,
    28→		`SELECT id, provider, repository FROM sources WHERE enabled = true`)
    29→	if err != nil {
    30→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("query enabled sources: %w", err)
    31→	}
    32→	defer rows.Close()
    33→
    34→	var sources []IIngestionSource
    35→	for rows.Next() {
    36→		var id string
    37→		var sourceType, repository string
    38→		if err := rows.Scan(&id, &sourceType, &repository); err != nil {
    39→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan source row: %w", err)
    40→		}
    41→
    42→		src := l.buildSource(id, sourceType, repository)
    43→		if src == nil {
    44→			slog.Warn("unsupported source type, skipping",
    45→				"id", id, "type", sourceType, "repo", repository)
    46→			continue
    47→		}
    48→		sources = append(sources, src)
    49→	}
    50→	return sources, rows.Err()
    51→}
    52→
    53→// LookupSourceID finds the source ID for a given (provider, repository) pair.
    54→// Returns empty string and false if no matching enabled source exists.
    55→func (l *SourceLoader) LookupSourceID(ctx context.Context, provider, repository string) (string, bool) {
    56→	var id string
    57→	err := l.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
    58→		`SELECT id FROM sources WHERE provider = $1 AND repository = $2 AND enabled = true`,
    59→		provider, repository,
    60→	).Scan(&id)
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		return "", false
    63→	}
    64→	return id, true
    65→}
    66→
    67→func (l *SourceLoader) buildSource(id string, sourceType, repository string) IIngestionSource {
    68→	switch sourceType {
    69→	case "dockerhub":
    70→		return NewDockerHubSource(l.client, repository, id)
    71→	case "github":
    72→		return NewGitHubAtomSource(l.client, repository, id)
    73→	default:
    74→		return nil
    75→	}
    76→}
    77→

    16→/* ---------- Inline Add Source Form ---------- */
    17→
    18→function InlineSourceForm({
    19→  projectId,
    20→  onDone,
    21→}: {
    22→  projectId: string;
    23→  onDone: () => void;
    24→}) {
    25→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("github");
    26→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState("");
    27→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState("300");
    28→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    29→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    30→
    31→  const handleAdd = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    32→    e.preventDefault();
    33→    if (!repository.trim()) return;
    34→    setSaving(true);
    35→    setError("");
    36→    try {
    37→      await sourcesApi.create(projectId, {
    38→        provider,
    39→        repository: repository.trim(),
    40→        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval) || 300,
    41→        enabled: true,
    42→      });
    43→      // Revalidate sources for this project
    44→      mutate(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`);
    45→      onDone();
    46→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    47→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to add source");
    48→    } finally {
    49→      setSaving(false);
    50→    }
    51→  };
    52→
    53→  return (
    54→    <form onSubmit={handleAdd} className="mt-2 rounded-md border p-3 space-y-2" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
    55→      {error && <div className="text-[12px] text-red-600">{error}</div>}
    56→      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
    57→        <select
    58→          value={provider}
    59→          onChange={(e) => setProvider(e.target.value)}
    60→          className="rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    61→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
    62→        >
    63→          <option value="github">GitHub</option>
    64→          <option value="dockerhub">Docker Hub</option>
    65→        </select>
    66→        <input
    67→          type="text"
    68→          value={repository}
    69→          onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)}
    70→          placeholder="e.g. ethereum/go-ethereum"
    71→          className="flex-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    72→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
    73→        />
    74→        <input
    75→          type="number"
    76→          min={60}
    77→          value={pollInterval}
    78→          onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)}
    79→          className="w-20 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    80→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
    81→          title="Poll interval (seconds)"
    82→        />
    83→      </div>
    84→      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
    85→        <button
    86→          type="button"
    87→          onClick={onDone}
    88→          className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
    89→        >
    90→          Cancel
    91→        </button>
    92→        <button
    93→          type="submit"
    94→          disabled={saving || !repository.trim()}
    95→          className="rounded-md px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40"
    96→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
    97→        >
    98→          {saving ? "Adding..." : "Add"}
    99→        </button>
   100→      </div>
   101→    </form>
   102→  );
   103→}

   130→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}

   142→          </button>

   145→
   146→      {sources.length > 0 ? (
   147→        <div className="space-y-1">
   148→          {sources.map((source) => {
   149→            const Icon = getProviderIcon(source.provider);
   150→            return (
   151→              <div
   152→                key={source.id}
   153→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px]"
   154→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}

   156→                {Icon && <Icon size={13} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />}
   157→                <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: "#374151" }}>

   172→                      : source.poll_interval_seconds < 3600

   175→                  </span>
   176→                </span>

   179→          })}
   180→        </div>

   186→
   187→      {adding && (

  "new_string": "function ProjectCard({ project }: { project: Project }) {\n  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);\n  const [name, setName] = useState(project.name);\n  const [description, setDescription] = useState(project.description ?? \"\");\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  const handleSave = async () => {\n    if (!name.trim()) return;\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await projectsApi.update(project.id, {\n        name: name.trim(),\n        description: description.trim() || undefined,\n      });\n      mutate(\"projects\");\n      setEditing(false);\n    } catch {\n      // keep editing on error\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleCancel = () => {\n    setName(project.name);\n    setDescription(project.description ?? \"\");\n    setEditing(false);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"overflow-hidden rounded-md\"\n      style={{ border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\", backgroundColor: \"#ffffff\" }}\n    >\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n        {editing ?      1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import {
     7→  projects as projectsApi,
     8→  releases as releasesApi,
     9→  sources as sourcesApi,
    10→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    11→import { getProviderIcon } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    12→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    13→import { Plus, X, Pencil, Check } from "lucide-react";
    14→import type { Project, Source, Release } from "@/lib/api/types";
    15→
    16→/* ---------- Inline Add Source Form ---------- */
    17→
    18→function InlineSourceForm({
    19→  projectId,
    20→  onDone,
    21→}: {
    22→  projectId: string;
    23→  onDone: () => void;
    24→}) {
    25→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("github");
    26→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState("");
    27→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState("300");
    28→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    29→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    30→
    31→  const handleAdd = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    32→    e.preventDefault();
    33→    if (!repository.trim()) return;
    34→    setSaving(true);
    35→    setError("");
    36→    try {
    37→      await sourcesApi.create(projectId, {
    38→        provider,
    39→        repository: repository.trim(),
    40→        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval) || 300,
    41→        enabled: true,
    42→      });
    43→      // Revalidate sources for this project
    44→      mutate(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`);
    45→      onDone();
    46→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    47→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to add source");
    48→    } finally {
    49→      setSaving(false);
    50→    }
    51→  };
    52→
    53→  return (
    54→    <form onSubmit={handleAdd} className="mt-2 rounded-md border p-3 space-y-2" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
    55→      {error && <div className="text-[12px] text-red-600">{error}</div>}
    56→      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
    57→        <select
    58→          value={provider}
    59→          onChange={(e) => setProvider(e.target.value)}
    60→          className="rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    61→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
    62→        >
    63→          <option value="github">GitHub</option>
    64→          <option value="dockerhub">Docker Hub</option>
    65→        </select>
    66→        <input
    67→          type="text"
    68→          value={repository}
    69→          onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)}
    70→          placeholder="e.g. ethereum/go-ethereum"
    71→          className="flex-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    72→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
    73→        />
    74→        <input
    75→          type="number"
    76→          min={60}
    77→          value={pollInterval}
    78→          onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)}
    79→          className="w-20 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    80→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
    81→          title="Poll interval (seconds)"
    82→        />
    83→      </div>
    84→      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
    85→        <button
    86→          type="button"
    87→          onClick={onDone}
    88→          className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
    89→        >
    90→          Cancel
    91→        </button>
    92→        <button
    93→          type="submit"
    94→          disabled={saving || !repository.trim()}
    95→          className="rounded-md px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40"
    96→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
    97→        >
    98→          {saving ? "Adding..." : "Add"}
    99→        </button>
   100→      </div>
   101→    </form>
   102→  );
   103→}
   104→
   105→/* ---------- Sources Section ---------- */
   106→
   107→function SourcesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   108→  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
   109→  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
   110→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
   111→  );
   112→  const sources = data?.data ?? [];
   113→
   114→  return (
   115→    <div
   116→      className="border-t px-4 py-3"
   117→      style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   118→    >
   119→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
   120→        <span
   121→          className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]"
   122→          style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   123→        >
   124→          Sources
   125→        </span>
   126→        {!adding && (
   127→          <button
   128→            onClick={() => setAdding(true)}
   129→            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors hover:opacity-80"
   130→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   131→          >
   132→            <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
   133→            Add Source
   134→          </button>
   135→        )}
   136→        {adding && (
   137→          <button
   138→            onClick={() => setAdding(false)}
   139→            className="text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
   140→          >
   141→            <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   142→          </button>
   143→        )}
   144→      </div>
   145→
   146→      {sources.length > 0 ? (
   147→        <div className="space-y-1">
   148→          {sources.map((source) => {
   149→            const Icon = getProviderIcon(source.provider);
   150→            return (
   151→              <div
   152→                key={source.id}
   153→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px]"
   154→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   155→              >
   156→                {Icon && <Icon size={13} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />}
   157→                <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: "#374151" }}>
   158→                  {source.repository}
   159→                </span>
   160→                <span
   161→                  className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]"
   162→                  style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   163→                >
   164→                  <span
   165→                    className="inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full"
   166→                    style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   167→                  />
   168→                  {source.enabled ? "Active" : "Disabled"}
   169→                  <span className="ml-1" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   170→                    {source.poll_interval_seconds < 60
   171→                      ? `${source.poll_interval_seconds}s`
   172→                      : source.poll_interval_seconds < 3600
   173→                        ? `${Math.round(source.poll_interval_seconds / 60)}m`
   174→                        : `${(source.poll_interval_seconds / 3600).toFixed(1)}h`}
   175→                  </span>
   176→                </span>
   177→              </div>
   178→            );
   179→          })}
   180→        </div>
   181→      ) : (
   182→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   183→          No sources configured
   184→        </p>
   185→      )}
   186→
   187→      {adding && (
   188→        <InlineSourceForm projectId={projectId} onDone={() => setAdding(false)} />
   189→      )}
   190→    </div>
   191→  );
   192→}
   193→
   194→/* ---------- Recent Releases Section ---------- */
   195→
   196→function RecentReleasesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   197→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-releases`, () =>
   198→    releasesApi.listByProject(projectId, 1, 5)
   199→  );
   200→  const { data: srcData } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
   201→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
   202→  );
   203→  const releases = relData?.data ?? [];
   204→  const sources = srcData?.data ?? [];
   205→
   206→  const sourceMap = new Map<string, Source>();
   207→  for (const s of sources) sourceMap.set(s.id, s);
   208→
   209→  return (
   210→    <div className="border-t px-4 py-3" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   211→      <span
   212→        className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mb-1 block"
   213→        style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   214→      >
   215→        Recent Releases
   216→      </span>
   217→
   218→      {releases.length > 0 ? (
   219→        <div className="space-y-1">
   220→          {releases.map((r) => {
   221→            const src = sourceMap.get(r.source_id);
   222→            const Icon = src ? getProviderIcon(src.provider) : undefined;
   223→            return (
   224→              <Link
   225→                key={r.id}
   226→                href={`/releases/${r.id}`}
   227→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] rounded px-1 -mx-1 py-0.5"
   228→              >
   229→                <span
   230→                  className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none"
   231→                  style={{
   232→                    backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1",
   233→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   234→                    color: "#374151",
   235→                  }}
   236→                >
   237→                  {r.version}
   238→                </span>
   239→                {Icon && <Icon size={11} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} />}
   240→                {src && (
   241→                  <span className="text-[11px] truncate" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   242→                    {src.repository}
   243→                  </span>
   244→                )}
   245→                <span className="ml-auto text-[11px] shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   246→                  {timeAgo(r.released_at || r.created_at)}
   247→                </span>
   248→              </Link>
   249→            );
   250→          })}
   251→          <Link
   252→            href={`/releases?project=${projectId}`}
   253→            className="block text-[11px] mt-1 hover:underline"
   254→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   255→          >
   256→            View all &rarr;
   257→          </Link>
   258→        </div>
   259→      ) : (
   260→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   261→          No releases yet
   262→        </p>
   263→      )}
   264→    </div>
   265→  );
   266→}
   267→
   268→/* ---------- Project Card ---------- */
   269→
   270→function ProjectCard({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   271→  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
   272→  const [name, setName] = useState(project.name);
   273→  const [description, setDescription] = useState(project.description ?? "");
   274→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   275→
   276→  const handleSave = async () => {
   277→    if (!name.trim()) return;
   278→    setSaving(true);
   279→    try {
   280→      await projectsApi.update(project.id, {
   281→        name: name.trim(),
   282→        description: description.trim() || undefined,
   283→      });
   284→      mutate("projects");
   285→      setEditing(false);
   286→    } catch {
   287→      // keep editing on error
   288→    } finally {
   289→      setSaving(false);
   290→    }
   291→  };
   292→
   293→  const handleCancel = () => {
   294→    setName(project.name);
   295→    setDescription(project.description ?? "");
   296→    setEditing(false);
   297→  };
   298→
   299→  return (
   300→    <div
   301→      className="overflow-hidden rounded-md max-w-2xl"
   302→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   303→    >
   304→      {/* Header */}
   305→      <div className="px-4 py-3">
   306→        {editing ? (
   307→          <div className="space-y-2 max-w-sm">
   308→            <input
   309→              type="text"
   310→              value={name}
   311→              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
   312→              className="w-full rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[16px] font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   313→              style={{
   314→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   315→                color: "#111113",
   316→                borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   317→              }}
   318→              autoFocus
   319→            />
   320→            <input
   321→              type="text"
   322→              value={description}
   323→              onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
   324→              placeholder="Description (optional)"
   325→              className="w-full rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[13px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   326→              style={{
   327→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   328→                color: "#6b7280",
   329→                borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   330→              }}
   331→            />
   332→            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   333→              <button
   334→                onClick={handleSave}
   335→                disabled={saving || !name.trim()}
   336→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40"
   337→                style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   338→              >
   339→                <Check className="h-3 w-3" />
   340→                {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}
   341→              </button>
   342→              <button
   343→                onClick={handleCancel}
   344→                className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
   345→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   346→              >
   347→                Cancel
   348→              </button>
   349→            </div>
   350→          </div>
   351→        ) : (
   352→          <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
   353→            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
   354→              <Link
   355→                href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
   356→                className="text-[16px] font-bold hover:underline"
   357→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#111113" }}
   358→              >
   359→                {project.name}
   360→              </Link>
   361→              {project.description && (
   362→                <p
   363→                  className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] truncate"
   364→                  style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   365→                >
   366→                  {project.description}
   367→                </p>
   368→              )}
   369→            </div>
   370→            <button
   371→              onClick={() => setEditing(true)}
   372→              className="ml-2 shrink-0 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   373→              style={{
   374→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   375→                borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   376→                color: "#6b7280",
   377→              }}
   378→            >
   379→              <Pencil className="h-3 w-3" />
   380→              Edit
   381→            </button>
   382→          </div>
   383→        )}
   384→      </div>
   385→
   386→      {/* Sources */}
   387→      <SourcesSection projectId={project.id} />
   388→
   389→      {/* Recent Releases */}
   390→      <RecentReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
   391→    </div>
   392→  );
   393→}
   394→
   395→/* ---------- Page ---------- */
   396→
   397→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   398→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list());
   399→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   400→
   401→  return (
   402→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   403→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   404→        <div>
   405→          <h1
   406→            style={{
   407→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   408→              fontSize: "24px",
   409→              fontWeight: 700,
   410→              color: "#111113",
   411→            }}
   412→          >
   413→            Projects
   414→          </h1>
   415→          <p
   416→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   417→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   418→          >
   419→            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.
   420→          </p>
   421→        </div>
   422→        <Link
   423→          href="/projects/new"
   424→          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   425→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   426→        >
   427→          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   428→          New Project
   429→        </Link>
   430→      </div>
   431→
   432→      {isLoading ? (
   433→        <p
   434→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   435→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   436→        >
   437→          Loading...
   438→        </p>
   439→      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   440→        <div
   441→          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-md border py-12"
   442→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   443→        >
   444→          <p
   445→            className="text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   446→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   447→          >
   448→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   449→          </p>
   450→          <Link
   451→            href="/projects/new"
   452→            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   453→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   454→          >
   455→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   456→            New Project
   457→          </Link>
   458→        </div>
   459→      ) : (
   460→        <div className="space-y-4">
   461→          {items.map((project) => (
   462→            <ProjectCard key={project.id} project={project} />
   463→          ))}
   464→        </div>
   465→      )}
   466→    </div>
   467→  );
   468→}

URGENCY_COLORS[sr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]\n              : undefined;\n            return (\n              <Link\n                key={sr.id}\n                href={`/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}\n                className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] rounded px-1 -mx-1 py-0.5\"\n              >\n                <span\n                  className=\"inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none\"\n                  style={{\n                    backgroundColor: \"#eff6ff\",\n                    fontFamily: \"'JetBrains Mono', monospace\",\n                    color: \"#1d4ed8\",\n                  }}\n                >\n                  {sr.version}\n                </span>\n                <span\n                  className=\"text-[11px]\"\n                  style={{ color: \"#9ca3af\" }}\n                >\n                  {sr.status}\n                </span>\n                {urgencyStyle && (\n                  <span\n                    className=\"inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-none\"\n                    style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}\n                  >\n                    {sr.report!.urgency}\n                  </span>\n                )}\n                {sr.report?.summary && (\n                  <span\n                    className=\"text-[11px] truncate flex-1\"\n                    style={{ color: \"#c4c4c0\" }}\n                  >\n                    {sr.report.summary.length > 60\n                      ? sr.report.summary.slice(0, 60) + \"\\u2026\"\n                      : sr.report.summary}\n                  </span>\n                )}\n                <span className=\"ml-auto text-[11px] shrink-0\" style={{ color: \"#c4c4c0\" }}>\n                  {timeAgo(sr.completed_at || sr.created_at)}\n                </span>\n              </Link>\n            );\n          })}\n          <Link\n            href={`/projects/${projectId}`}\n            className=\"block text-[11px] mt-1 hover:underline\"\n            style={{ color: \"#e8601a\", fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n          >\n            View all &rarr;\n          </Link>\n        </div>\n      ) : (\n        <p className=\"text-[12px] italic\" style={{ color: \"#c4c4c0\" }}>\n          No semantic releases yet\n        </p>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n/* ---------- Project Card ---------- */\n\nfunction ProjectCard({ project }: { project: Project }) {\n  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);\n  const [name, setName] = useState(project.name);\n  const [description, setDescription] = useState(project.description ?? \"\");\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  const handleSave = async () => {\n    if (!name.trim()) return;\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await projectsApi.update(project.id, {\n        name: name.trim(),\n        description: description.trim() || undefined,\n      });\n      mutate(\"projects\");\n      setEditing(false);\n    } catch {\n      // keep editing on error\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleCancel = () => {\n    setName(project.name);\n    setDescription(project.description ?? (\n          <div className=\"space-y-2 max-w-sm\">\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              value={name}\n              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}\n              className=\"w-full rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[16px] font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-1\"\n              style={{\n                fontFamily: \"var(--font-fraunces)\",\n                color: \"#111113\",\n                borderColor: \"#e8e8e5\",\n              }}\n              autoFocus\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              value={description}\n              onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}\n              placeholder=\"Description (optional)\"\n              className=\"w-full rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[13px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1\"\n              style={{\n                fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                color: \"#6b7280\",\n                borderColor: \"#e8e8e5\",\n              }}\n            />\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n              <button\n                onClick={handleSave}\n                disabled={saving || !name.trim()}\n                className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40\"\n                style={{ backgroundColor: \"#e8601a\", fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n              >\n                <Check className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n                {saving ? \"Saving...\" : \"Save\"}\n              </button>\n              <button\n                onClick={handleCancel}\n                className=\"text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]\"\n                style={{ fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n              >\n                Cancel\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between\">\n            <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n              <Link\n                href={`/projects/${project.id}`}\n                className=\"text-[16px] font-bold hover:underline\"\n                style={{ fontFamily: \"var(--font-fraunces)\", color: \"#111113\" }}\n              >\n                {project.name}\n              </Link>\n              {project.description && (\n                <p\n                  className=\"mt-0.5 text-[13px] truncate\"\n                  style={{ color: \"#6b7280\", fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n                >\n                  {project.description}\n                </p>\n              )}\n            </div>\n            <button\n              onClick={() => setEditing(true)}\n              className=\"ml-2 shrink-0 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]\"\n              style={{\n                fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                borderColor: \"#e8e8e5\",\n                color: \"#6b7280\",\n              }}\n            >\n              <Pencil className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n              Edit\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Sources */}\n      <SourcesSection projectId={project.id} />\n\n      {/* Recent Releases */}\n      <RecentReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />\n\n      {/* Semantic Releases */}\n      <SemanticReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n/* ---------- Page ---------- */\n\nexport default function ProjectsPage() {\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(\"projects\", () => projectsApi.list());\n  const items = data?.data ?? [];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in max-w-2xl\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <div>\n          <h1\n            style={{\n              fontFamily: \"var(--font-fraunces)\",\n              fontSize: \"24px\",\n              fontWeight: 700,\n              color: \"#111113\",\n            }}\n          >\n            Projects\n          </h1>\n          <p\n            className=\"mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]\"\n            style={{ fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n          >\n            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <Link\n          href=\"/projects/new\"\n          className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90\"\n          style={{ backgroundColor: \"#e8601a\", fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n        >\n          <Plus className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n          New Project\n        </Link>\n      </div>\n\n      {isLoading ? (\n        <p\n          className=\"px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]\"\n          style={{ fontFamily: \"var(--font-fraunces)\" }}\n        >\n          Loading...\n        </p>\n      ) : items.length === 0 ?
