    17→/* ---------- Inline Add Source Form ---------- */
    18→
    19→function InlineSourceForm({
    20→  projectId,
    21→  onDone,
    22→}: {
    23→  projectId: string;
    24→  onDone: () => void;
    25→}) {
    26→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("github");
    27→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState("");
    28→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState("300");
    29→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    30→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    31→
    32→  const handleAdd = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    33→    e.preventDefault();
    34→    if (!repository.trim()) return;
    35→    setSaving(true);
    36→    setError("");
    37→    try {
    38→      await sourcesApi.create(projectId, {
    39→        provider,
    40→        repository: repository.trim(),
    41→        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval) || 300,
    42→        enabled: true,
    43→      });
    44→      mutate(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`);
    45→      onDone();
    46→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    47→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to add source");
    48→    } finally {
    49→      setSaving(false);
    50→    }
    51→  };
    52→
    53→  return (
    54→    <form onSubmit={handleAdd} className="mt-2 rounded-md border p-3 space-y-2" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
    55→      {error && <div className="text-[12px] text-red-600">{error}</div>}
    56→      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
    57→        <select
    58→          value={provider}
    59→          onChange={(e) => setProvider(e.target.value)}
    60→          className="rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    61→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
    62→        >
    63→          <option value="github">GitHub</option>
    64→          <option value="dockerhub">Docker Hub</option>
    65→        </select>
    66→        <input
    67→          type="text"
    68→          value={repository}
    69→          onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)}
    70→          placeholder="e.g. ethereum/go-ethereum"
    71→          className="flex-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    72→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
    73→        />
    74→        <input
    75→          type="number"
    76→          min={60}
    77→          value={pollInterval}
    78→          onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)}
    79→          className="w-20 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    80→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
    81→          title="Poll interval (seconds)"
    82→        />
    83→      </div>
    84→      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
    85→        <button
    86→          type="button"
    87→          onClick={onDone}
    88→          className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
    89→        >
    90→          Cancel
    91→        </button>
    92→        <button
    93→          type="submit"
    94→          disabled={saving || !repository.trim()}
    95→          className="rounded-md px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40"
    96→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
    97→        >
    98→          {saving ? "Adding..." : "Add"}
    99→        </button>
   100→      </div>
   101→    </form>
   102→  );
   103→}
   104→
   105→/* ---------- Sources Section ---------- */
   106→
   107→function SourcesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   108→  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
   109→  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
   110→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
   111→  );
   112→  const sources = data?.data ?? [];
   113→
   114→  return (
   115→    <div className="border-t px-4 py-3" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   116→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
   117→        <span
   118→          className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]"
   119→          style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   120→        >
   121→          Sources
   122→        </span>
   123→        {!adding && (
   124→          <button
   125→            onClick={() => setAdding(true)}
   126→            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors hover:opacity-80"
   127→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   128→          >
   129→            <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
   130→            Add Source
   131→          </button>
   132→        )}
   133→        {adding && (
   134→          <button
   135→            onClick={() => setAdding(false)}
   136→            className="text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
   137→          >
   138→            <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   139→          </button>
   140→        )}
   141→      </div>
   142→
   143→      {sources.length > 0 ? (
   144→        <div className="space-y-1">
   145→          {sources.map((source) => {
   146→            const Icon = getProviderIcon(source.provider);
   147→            return (
   148→              <div
   149→                key={source.id}
   150→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px]"
   151→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   152→              >
   153→                {Icon && <Icon size={13} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />}
   154→                <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: "#374151" }}>
   155→                  {source.repository}
   156→                </span>
   157→                <span
   158→                  className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]"
   159→                  style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   160→                >
   161→                  <span
   162→                    className="inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full"
   163→                    style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   164→                  />
   165→                  {source.enabled ? "Active" : "Disabled"}
   166→                  <span className="ml-1" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   167→                    {source.poll_interval_seconds < 60
   168→                      ? `${source.poll_interval_seconds}s`
   169→                      : source.poll_interval_seconds < 3600
   170→                        ? `${Math.round(source.poll_interval_seconds / 60)}m`
   171→                        : `${(source.poll_interval_seconds / 3600).toFixed(1)}h`}
   172→                  </span>
   173→                </span>
   174→              </div>
   175→            );
   176→          })}
   177→        </div>
   178→      ) : (
   179→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   180→          No sources configured
   181→        </p>
   182→      )}
   183→
   184→      {adding && (
   185→        <InlineSourceForm projectId={projectId} onDone={() => setAdding(false)} />
   186→      )}
   187→    </div>
   188→  );
   189→}
   190→
   191→/* ---------- Recent Releases Section ---------- */
   192→
   193→function RecentReleasesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   194→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-releases`, () =>
   195→    releasesApi.listByProject(projectId, 1, 5)
   196→  );
   197→  const { data: srcData } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
   198→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
   199→  );
   200→  const releases = relData?.data ?? [];
   201→  const sources = srcData?.data ?? [];
   202→
   203→  const sourceMap = new Map<string, Source>();
   204→  for (const s of sources) sourceMap.set(s.id, s);
   205→
   206→  return (
   207→    <div className="border-t px-4 py-3" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   208→      <span
   209→        className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mb-1 block"
   210→        style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   211→      >
   212→        Recent Releases
   213→      </span>
   214→
   215→      {releases.length > 0 ? (
   216→        <div className="space-y-1">
   217→          {releases.map((r) => {
   218→            const src = sourceMap.get(r.source_id);
   219→            const Icon = src ? getProviderIcon(src.provider) : undefined;
   220→            return (
   221→              <Link
   222→                key={r.id}
   223→                href={`/releases/${r.id}`}
   224→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] rounded px-1 -mx-1 py-0.5"
   225→              >
   226→                <span
   227→                  className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none"
   228→                  style={{
   229→                    backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1",
   230→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   231→                    color: "#374151",
   232→                  }}
   233→                >
   234→                  {r.version}
   235→                </span>
   236→                {Icon && <Icon size={11} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} />}
   237→                {src && (
   238→                  <span className="text-[11px] truncate" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   239→                    {src.repository}
   240→                  </span>
   241→                )}
   242→                <span className="ml-auto text-[11px] shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   243→                  {timeAgo(r.released_at || r.created_at)}
   244→                </span>
   245→              </Link>
   246→            );
   247→          })}
   248→          <Link
   249→            href={`/releases?project=${projectId}`}
   250→            className="block text-[11px] mt-1 hover:underline"
   251→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   252→          >
   253→            View all &rarr;
   254→          </Link>
   255→        </div>
   256→      ) : (
   257→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   258→          No releases yet
   259→        </p>
   260→      )}
   261→    </div>
   262→  );
   263→}
   264→

   326→                    style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}
   327→                  >
   328→                    {sr.report.summary.length > 60
   329→                      ? sr.report.summary.slice(0, 60) + "\u2026"
   330→                      : sr.report.summary}
   331→                  </span>
   332→                )}
   333→                <span className="ml-auto text-[11px] shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   334→                  {timeAgo(sr.completed_at || sr.created_at)}
   335→                </span>
   336→              </Link>
   337→            );
   338→          })}
   339→          <Link
   340→            href={`/projects/${projectId}`}
   341→            className="block text-[11px] mt-1 hover:underline"
   342→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   343→          >
   344→            View all &rarr;
   345→          </Link>
   346→        </div>
   347→      ) : (
   348→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   349→          No semantic releases yet
   350→        </p>
   351→      )}
   352→    </div>
   353→  );
   354→}
   355→
   356→/* ---------- Project Card ---------- */
   357→
   358→function ProjectCard({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   359→  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
   360→  const [name, setName] = useState(project.name);
   361→  const [description, setDescription] = useState(project.description ?? "");
   362→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   363→
   364→  const handleSave = async () => {
   365→    if (!name.trim()) return;
   366→    setSaving(true);
   367→    try {
   368→      await projectsApi.update(project.id, {
   369→        name: name.trim(),
   370→        description: description.trim() || undefined,
   371→      });
   372→      mutate("projects");
   373→      setEditing(false);
   374→    } catch {
   375→      // keep editing on error
   376→    } finally {
   377→      setSaving(false);
   378→    }
   379→  };
   380→
   381→  const handleCancel = () => {
   382→    setName(project.name);
   383→    setDescription(project.description ?? "");
   384→    setEditing(false);
   385→  };
   386→
   387→  return (
   388→    <div
   389→      className="overflow-hidden rounded-md"
   390→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   391→    >
   392→      {/* Header */}
   393→      <div className="px-4 py-3">
   394→        {editing ? (
   395→          <div className="space-y-2 max-w-sm">
   396→            <input
   397→              type="text"
   398→              value={name}
   399→              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
   400→              className="w-full rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[16px] font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   401→              style={{
   402→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   403→                color: "#111113",
   404→                borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   405→              }}
   406→              autoFocus
   407→            />
   408→            <input
   409→              type="text"
   410→              value={description}
   411→              onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
   412→              placeholder="Description (optional)"
   413→              className="w-full rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[13px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   414→              style={{
   415→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   416→                color: "#6b7280",
   417→                borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   418→              }}
   419→            />
   420→            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   421→              <button
   422→                onClick={handleSave}
   423→                disabled={saving || !name.trim()}
   424→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40"
   425→                style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   426→              >
   427→                <Check className="h-3 w-3" />
   428→                {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}
   429→              </button>
   430→              <button
   431→                onClick={handleCancel}
   432→                className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
   433→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   434→              >
   435→                Cancel
   436→              </button>
   437→            </div>
   438→          </div>
   439→        ) : (
   440→          <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
   441→            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
   442→              <Link
   443→                href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
   444→                className="text-[16px] font-bold hover:underline"
   445→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#111113" }}
   446→              >
   447→                {project.name}
   448→              </Link>
   449→              {project.description && (
   450→                <p
   451→                  className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] truncate"
   452→                  style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   453→                >
   454→                  {project.description}
   455→                </p>
   456→              )}
   457→            </div>
   458→            <button
   459→              onClick={() => setEditing(true)}
   460→              className="ml-2 shrink-0 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   461→              style={{
   462→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   463→                borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   464→                color: "#6b7280",
   465→              }}
   466→            >
   467→              <Pencil className="h-3 w-3" />
   468→              Edit
   469→            </button>
   470→          </div>
   471→        )}
   472→      </div>
   473→
   474→      {/* Sources */}
   475→      <SourcesSection projectId={project.id} />
   476→
   477→      {/* Recent Releases */}
   478→      <RecentReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
   479→
   480→      {/* Semantic Releases */}
   481→      <SemanticReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
   482→    </div>
   483→  );
   484→}
   485→
   486→/* ---------- Page ---------- */
   487→
   488→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   489→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list());
   490→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   491→
   492→  return (
   493→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in max-w-2xl">
   494→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   495→        <div>
   496→          <h1
   497→            style={{
   498→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   499→              fontSize: "24px",
   500→              fontWeight: 700,
   501→              color: "#111113",
   502→            }}
   503→          >
   504→            Projects
   505→          </h1>
   506→          <p
   507→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   508→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   509→          >
   510→            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.
   511→          </p>
   512→        </div>
   513→        <Link
   514→          href="/projects/new"
   515→          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   516→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   517→        >
   518→          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   519→          New Project
   520→        </Link>
   521→      </div>
   522→
   523→      {isLoading ? (
   524→        <p
   525→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   526→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   527→        >
   528→          Loading...
   529→        </p>
   530→      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   531→        <div
   532→          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-md border py-12"
   533→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   534→        >
   535→          <p
   536→            className="text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   537→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   538→          >
   539→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   540→          </p>
   541→          <Link
   542→            href="/projects/new"
   543→            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   544→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   545→          >
   546→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   547→            New Project
   548→          </Link>
   549→        </div>
   550→      ) : (
   551→        <div className="space-y-4">
   552→          {items.map((project) => (
   553→            <ProjectCard key={project.id} project={project} />

   555→        </div>
   556→      )}
   557→    </div>
   558→  );
   559→}

    21→const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "/api/v1";
    22→
    23→async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    24→  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    25→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...init?.headers },
    26→    ...init,
    27→  });
    28→  if (!res.ok) {
    29→    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    30→    throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? `Request failed: ${res.status}`);
    31→  }
    32→  return res.json();
    33→}
    34→

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import {
     7→  projects as projectsApi,
     8→  releases as releasesApi,
     9→  sources as sourcesApi,
    10→  semanticReleases as srApi,
    11→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    12→import { getProviderIcon } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    13→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    14→import { Plus, X, Pencil, Check } from "lucide-react";
    15→import type { Project, Source } from "@/lib/api/types";
    16→
    17→/* ---------- Inline Add Source Form ---------- */
    18→
    19→function InlineSourceForm({
    20→  projectId,
    21→  onDone,
    22→}: {
    23→  projectId: string;
    24→  onDone: () => void;
    25→}) {
    26→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("github");
    27→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState("");
    28→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState("300");
    29→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    30→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    31→
    32→  const handleAdd = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    33→    e.preventDefault();
    34→    if (!repository.trim()) return;
    35→    setSaving(true);
    36→    setError("");
    37→    try {
    38→      await sourcesApi.create(projectId, {
    39→        provider,
    40→        repository: repository.trim(),
    41→        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval) || 300,
    42→        enabled: true,
    43→      });
    44→      mutate(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`);
    45→      onDone();
    46→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    47→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to add source");
    48→    } finally {
    49→      setSaving(false);
    50→    }
    51→  };
    52→
    53→  return (
    54→    <form onSubmit={handleAdd} className="mt-2 rounded-md border p-3 space-y-2" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
    55→      {error && <div className="text-[12px] text-red-600">{error}</div>}
    56→      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
    57→        <select
    58→          value={provider}
    59→          onChange={(e) => setProvider(e.target.value)}
    60→          className="rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    61→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
    62→        >
    63→          <option value="github">GitHub</option>
    64→          <option value="dockerhub">Docker Hub</option>
    65→        </select>
    66→        <input
    67→          type="text"
    68→          value={repository}
    69→          onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)}
    70→          placeholder="e.g. ethereum/go-ethereum"
    71→          className="flex-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    72→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
    73→        />
    74→        <input
    75→          type="number"
    76→          min={60}
    77→          value={pollInterval}
    78→          onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)}
    79→          className="w-20 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    80→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
    81→          title="Poll interval (seconds)"
    82→        />
    83→      </div>
    84→      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
    85→        <button
    86→          type="button"
    87→          onClick={onDone}
    88→          className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
    89→        >
    90→          Cancel
    91→        </button>
    92→        <button
    93→          type="submit"
    94→          disabled={saving || !repository.trim()}
    95→          className="rounded-md px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40"
    96→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
    97→        >
    98→          {saving ? "Adding..." : "Add"}
    99→        </button>
   100→      </div>
   101→    </form>
   102→  );
   103→}
   104→
   105→/* ---------- Sources Section ---------- */
   106→
   107→function SourcesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   108→  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
   109→  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
   110→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
   111→  );
   112→  const sources = data?.data ?? [];
   113→
   114→  return (
   115→    <div className="px-4 py-3 md:border-r" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   116→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
   117→        <span
   118→          className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]"
   119→          style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   120→        >
   121→          Sources
   122→        </span>
   123→        {!adding && (
   124→          <button
   125→            onClick={() => setAdding(true)}
   126→            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors hover:opacity-80"
   127→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   128→          >
   129→            <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
   130→            Add Source
   131→          </button>
   132→        )}
   133→        {adding && (
   134→          <button
   135→            onClick={() => setAdding(false)}
   136→            className="text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
   137→          >
   138→            <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   139→          </button>
   140→        )}
   141→      </div>
   142→
   143→      {sources.length > 0 ? (
   144→        <div className="space-y-1">
   145→          {sources.map((source) => {
   146→            const Icon = getProviderIcon(source.provider);
   147→            return (
   148→              <div
   149→                key={source.id}
   150→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px]"
   151→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   152→              >
   153→                {Icon && <Icon size={13} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />}
   154→                <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: "#374151" }}>
   155→                  {source.repository}
   156→                </span>
   157→                <span
   158→                  className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]"
   159→                  style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   160→                >
   161→                  <span
   162→                    className="inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full"
   163→                    style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   164→                  />
   165→                  {source.enabled ? "Active" : "Disabled"}
   166→                  <span className="ml-1" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   167→                    {source.poll_interval_seconds < 60
   168→                      ? `${source.poll_interval_seconds}s`
   169→                      : source.poll_interval_seconds < 3600
   170→                        ? `${Math.round(source.poll_interval_seconds / 60)}m`
   171→                        : `${(source.poll_interval_seconds / 3600).toFixed(1)}h`}
   172→                  </span>
   173→                </span>
   174→              </div>
   175→            );
   176→          })}
   177→        </div>
   178→      ) : (
   179→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   180→          No sources configured
   181→        </p>
   182→      )}
   183→
   184→      {adding && (
   185→        <InlineSourceForm projectId={projectId} onDone={() => setAdding(false)} />
   186→      )}
   187→    </div>
   188→  );
   189→}
   190→
   191→/* ---------- Recent Releases Section ---------- */
   192→
   193→function RecentReleasesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   194→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-releases`, () =>
   195→    releasesApi.listByProject(projectId, 1, 5)
   196→  );
   197→  const { data: srcData } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
   198→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
   199→  );
   200→  const releases = relData?.data ?? [];
   201→  const sources = srcData?.data ?? [];
   202→
   203→  const sourceMap = new Map<string, Source>();
   204→  for (const s of sources) sourceMap.set(s.id, s);
   205→
   206→  return (
   207→    <div className="border-t md:border-t-0 md:border-r px-4 py-3" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   208→      <span
   209→        className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mb-1 block"
   210→        style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   211→      >
   212→        Recent Releases
   213→      </span>
   214→
   215→      {releases.length > 0 ? (
   216→        <div className="space-y-1">
   217→          {releases.map((r) => {
   218→            const src = sourceMap.get(r.source_id);
   219→            const Icon = src ? getProviderIcon(src.provider) : undefined;
   220→            return (
   221→              <Link
   222→                key={r.id}
   223→                href={`/releases/${r.id}`}
   224→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] rounded px-1 -mx-1 py-0.5"
   225→              >
   226→                <span
   227→                  className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none"
   228→                  style={{
   229→                    backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1",
   230→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   231→                    color: "#374151",
   232→                  }}
   233→                >
   234→                  {r.version}
   235→                </span>
   236→                {Icon && <Icon size={11} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} />}
   237→                {src && (
   238→                  <span className="text-[11px] truncate" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   239→                    {src.repository}
   240→                  </span>
   241→                )}
   242→                <span className="ml-auto text-[11px] shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   243→                  {timeAgo(r.released_at || r.created_at)}
   244→                </span>
   245→              </Link>
   246→            );
   247→          })}
   248→          <Link
   249→            href={`/releases?project=${projectId}`}
   250→            className="block text-[11px] mt-1 hover:underline"
   251→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   252→          >
   253→            View all &rarr;
   254→          </Link>
   255→        </div>
   256→      ) : (
   257→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   258→          No releases yet
   259→        </p>
   260→      )}
   261→    </div>
   262→  );
   263→}
   264→
   265→/* ---------- Semantic Releases Section ---------- */
   266→
   267→const URGENCY_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {
   268→  critical: { bg: "#dc2626", text: "#ffffff" },
   269→  high: { bg: "#f97316", text: "#ffffff" },
   270→};
   271→
   272→function SemanticReleasesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   273→  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sr`, () =>
   274→    srApi.list(projectId, 1, 3)
   275→  );
   276→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   277→
   278→  return (
   279→    <div className="border-t md:border-t-0 px-4 py-3" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   280→      <span
   281→        className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mb-1 block"
   282→        style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   283→      >
   284→        Semantic Releases
   285→      </span>
   286→
   287→      {items.length > 0 ? (
   288→        <div className="space-y-1">
   289→          {items.map((sr) => {
   290→            const urgencyStyle = sr.report?.urgency
   291→              ? URGENCY_COLORS[sr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   292→              : undefined;
   293→            return (
   294→              <Link
   295→                key={sr.id}
   296→                href={`/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}
   297→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] rounded px-1 -mx-1 py-0.5"
   298→              >
   299→                <span
   300→                  className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none"
   301→                  style={{
   302→                    backgroundColor: "#eff6ff",
   303→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   304→                    color: "#1d4ed8",
   305→                  }}
   306→                >
   307→                  {sr.version}
   308→                </span>
   309→                <span
   310→                  className="text-[11px]"
   311→                  style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   312→                >
   313→                  {sr.status}
   314→                </span>
   315→                {urgencyStyle && (
   316→                  <span
   317→                    className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-none"
   318→                    style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}
   319→                  >
   320→                    {sr.report!.urgency}
   321→                  </span>
   322→                )}
   323→                {sr.report?.summary && (
   324→                  <span
   325→                    className="text-[11px] truncate flex-1"
   326→                    style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}
   327→                  >
   328→                    {sr.report.summary.length > 60
   329→                      ? sr.report.summary.slice(0, 60) + "\u2026"
   330→                      : sr.report.summary}
   331→                  </span>
   332→                )}
   333→                <span className="ml-auto text-[11px] shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   334→                  {timeAgo(sr.completed_at || sr.created_at)}
   335→                </span>
   336→              </Link>
   337→            );
   338→          })}
   339→          <Link
   340→            href={`/projects/${projectId}`}
   341→            className="block text-[11px] mt-1 hover:underline"
   342→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   343→          >
   344→            View all &rarr;
   345→          </Link>
   346→        </div>
   347→      ) : (
   348→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   349→          No semantic releases yet
   350→        </p>
   351→      )}
   352→    </div>
   353→  );
   354→}
   355→
   356→/* ---------- Project Card ---------- */
   357→
   358→function ProjectCard({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   359→  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
   360→  const [name, setName] = useState(project.name);
   361→  const [description, setDescription] = useState(project.description ?? "");
   362→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   363→
   364→  const handleSave = async () => {
   365→    if (!name.trim()) return;
   366→    setSaving(true);
   367→    try {
   368→      await projectsApi.update(project.id, {
   369→        name: name.trim(),
   370→        description: description.trim() || undefined,
   371→      });
   372→      mutate("projects");
   373→      setEditing(false);
   374→    } catch {
   375→      // keep editing on error
   376→    } finally {
   377→      setSaving(false);
   378→    }
   379→  };
   380→
   381→  const handleCancel = () => {
   382→    setName(project.name);
   383→    setDescription(project.description ?? "");
   384→    setEditing(false);
   385→  };
   386→
   387→  return (
   388→    <div
   389→      className="overflow-hidden rounded-md"
   390→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   391→    >
   392→      {/* Header */}
   393→      <div className="px-4 py-3">
   394→        {editing ? (
   395→          <div className="space-y-2 max-w-sm">
   396→            <input
   397→              type="text"
   398→              value={name}
   399→              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
   400→              className="w-full rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[16px] font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   401→              style={{
   402→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   403→                color: "#111113",
   404→                borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   405→              }}
   406→              autoFocus
   407→            />
   408→            <input
   409→              type="text"
   410→              value={description}
   411→              onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
   412→              placeholder="Description (optional)"
   413→              className="w-full rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[13px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   414→              style={{
   415→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   416→                color: "#6b7280",
   417→                borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   418→              }}
   419→            />
   420→            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   421→              <button
   422→                onClick={handleSave}
   423→                disabled={saving || !name.trim()}
   424→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40"
   425→                style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   426→              >
   427→                <Check className="h-3 w-3" />
   428→                {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}
   429→              </button>
   430→              <button
   431→                onClick={handleCancel}
   432→                className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-[#6b7280]"
   433→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   434→              >
   435→                Cancel
   436→              </button>
   437→            </div>
   438→          </div>
   439→        ) : (
   440→          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   441→            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
   442→              <Link
   443→                href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
   444→                className="text-[16px] font-bold hover:underline"
   445→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#111113" }}
   446→              >
   447→                {project.name}
   448→              </Link>
   449→              {project.description && (
   450→                <p
   451→                  className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] truncate"
   452→                  style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   453→                >
   454→                  {project.description}
   455→                </p>
   456→              )}
   457→            </div>
   458→            <button
   459→              onClick={() => setEditing(true)}
   460→              className="shrink-0 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   461→              style={{
   462→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   463→                borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   464→                color: "#6b7280",
   465→              }}
   466→            >
   467→              <Pencil className="h-3 w-3" />
   468→              Edit
   469→            </button>
   470→          </div>
   471→        )}
   472→      </div>
   473→
   474→      {/* Content columns: sources | recent releases | semantic releases */}
   475→      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   476→        <SourcesSection projectId={project.id} />
   477→        <RecentReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
   478→        <SemanticReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
   479→      </div>
   480→    </div>
   481→  );
   482→}
   483→
   484→/* ---------- Page ---------- */
   485→
   486→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   487→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list());
   488→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   489→
   490→  return (
   491→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   492→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   493→        <div>
   494→          <h1
   495→            style={{
   496→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   497→              fontSize: "24px",
   498→              fontWeight: 700,
   499→              color: "#111113",
   500→            }}
   501→          >
   502→            Projects
   503→          </h1>
   504→          <p
   505→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   506→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   507→          >
   508→            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.
   509→          </p>
   510→        </div>
   511→        <Link
   512→          href="/projects/new"
   513→          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   514→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   515→        >
   516→          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   517→          New Project
   518→        </Link>
   519→      </div>
   520→
   521→      {isLoading ? (
   522→        <p
   523→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   524→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   525→        >
   526→          Loading...
   527→        </p>
   528→      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   529→        <div
   530→          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-md border py-12"
   531→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   532→        >
   533→          <p
   534→            className="text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   535→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   536→          >
   537→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   538→          </p>
   539→          <Link
   540→            href="/projects/new"
   541→            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   542→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   543→          >
   544→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   545→            New Project
   546→          </Link>
   547→        </div>
   548→      ) : (
   549→        <div className="space-y-4">
   550→          {items.map((project) => (
   551→            <ProjectCard key={project.id} project={project} />
   552→          ))}
   553→        </div>
   554→      )}
   555→    </div>
   556→  );
   557→}
   558→

**ProjectEdit Component:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/projects/project-edit.tsx`

**ProjectForm Component:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/projects/project-form.tsx`

- Has optional source section (hidden in edit mode via `hideSource` prop)

     5→import { useState } from "react";

     8→  projects as projectsApi,

    20→
    21→/* ---------- Tabs ---------- */
    22→
    23→const tabs = [
    24→  { key: "sources", label: "Sources" },
    25→  { key: "context", label: "Context Sources" },
    26→  { key: "semantic", label: "Semantic Releases" },
    27→  { key: "agent", label: "Agent" },
    28→] as const;
    29→
    30→type TabKey = (typeof tabs)[number]["key"];
    31→
    32→/* ---------- Helpers ---------- */
    33→
    34→function formatDuration(startedAt?: string, completedAt?: string): string {
    35→  if (!startedAt) return "--";
    36→  const start = new Date(startedAt).getTime();
    37→  const end = completedAt ? new Date(completedAt).getTime() : Date.now();
    38→  const secs = Math.round((end - start) / 1000);
    39→  if (secs < 60) return `${secs}s`;
    40→  const mins = Math.floor(secs / 60);
    41→  const rem = secs % 60;
    42→  return `${mins}m ${rem}s`;
    43→}
    44→
    45→function truncate(str: string, max: number): string {
    46→  return str.length > max ? str.slice(0, max) + "\u2026" : str;
    47→}
    48→
    49→function formatInterval(seconds: number): string {
    50→  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;
    51→  if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.round(seconds / 60)}m`;
    52→  return `${(seconds / 3600).toFixed(1)}h`;
    53→}
    54→
    55→/* ---------- Urgency chip ---------- */
    56→
    57→const URGENCY_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {
    58→  critical: { bg: "#dc2626", text: "#ffffff" },
    59→  high: { bg: "#f97316", text: "#ffffff" },
    60→};
    61→
    62→function UrgencyChip({ urgency }: { urgency: string }) {
    63→  const u = urgency.toLowerCase();
    64→  const style = URGENCY_COLORS[u];
    65→  if (!style) return null;
    66→  return (
    67→    <span
    68→      className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none"
    69→      style={{ backgroundColor: style.bg, color: style.text }}
    70→    >
    71→      {urgency}
    72→    </span>
    73→  );
    74→}
    75→
    76→/* ---------- Main component ---------- */
    77→
    78→export function ProjectDetail({ id }: { id: string }) {
    79→  const router = useRouter();
    80→  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabKey>("sources");
    81→  const [triggering, setTriggering] = useState(false);

    91→    activeTab === "sources" ? `project-${id}-sources` : null,

    94→  const { data: ctxData, mutate: mutateCtx } = useSWR(
    95→    activeTab === "context" ? `project-${id}-ctx` : null,
    96→    () => ctxApi.list(id),
    97→  );
    98→  const { data: srData } = useSWR(
    99→    activeTab === "semantic" ? `project-${id}-sr` : null,
   100→    () => srApi.list(id),
   101→  );
   102→  const { data: runsData, mutate: mutateRuns } = useSWR(
   103→    activeTab === "agent" ? `project-${id}-runs` : null,
   104→    () => agentApi.listRuns(id),
   105→  );
   106→
   107→  /* Handlers */
   108→  const handleDelete = async () => {
   109→    if (!confirm("Delete this project? This will cascade to sources and subscriptions.")) return;
   110→    await projectsApi.delete(id);
   111→    router.push("/projects");
   112→  };
   113→
   114→  const handleTriggerRun = async () => {
   115→    setTriggering(true);
   116→    try {
   117→      await agentApi.triggerRun(id);
   118→      mutateRuns();
   119→    } finally {
   120→      setTriggering(false);
   121→    }
   122→  };
   123→
   124→  const handleDeleteSource = async (sourceId: string) => {
   125→    if (!confirm("Delete this source?")) return;
   126→    await sourcesApi.delete(sourceId);
   127→    mutateSources();
   128→  };
   129→
   130→  const handleDeleteCtx = async (ctxId: string) => {
   131→    if (!confirm("Delete this context source?")) return;
   132→    await ctxApi.delete(ctxId);
   133→    mutateCtx();
   134→  };
   135→
   136→  const handleToggleSource = async (source: { id: string; provider: string; repository: string; poll_interval_seconds: number; enabled: boolean; config?: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
   137→    await sourcesApi.update(source.id, {
   138→      provider: source.provider,
   139→      repository: source.repository,
   140→      poll_interval_seconds: source.poll_interval_seconds,
   141→      enabled: !source.enabled,
   142→      config: source.config,
   143→    });
   144→    mutateSources();
   145→  };
   146→
   147→  const handleSaveAgentConfig = async () => {
   148→    if (!project) return;
   149→    setSaving(true);
   150→    try {
   151→      const input = {
   152→        name: project.name,
   153→        description: project.description,
   154→        agent_prompt: promptDraft ?? project.agent_prompt,
   155→        agent_rules: rulesDraft ?? project.agent_rules,
   156→      };
   157→      await projectsApi.update(id, input);
   158→      mutateProject();
   159→      setPromptDraft(null);
   160→      setRulesDraft(null);
   161→    } finally {
   162→      setSaving(false);
   163→    }
   164→  };
   165→
   166→  /* Loading / not found states */
   167→  if (isLoading) {
   168→    return (
   169→      <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   170→        Loading...
   171→      </div>
   172→    );
   173→  }
   174→
   175→  const project = data?.data;
   176→  if (!project) {
   177→    return (
   178→      <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>
   179→        Project not found
   180→      </div>
   181→    );
   182→  }
   183→
   184→  /* Derived counts */
   185→  const sourceCount = sourcesData?.data?.length;
   186→  const ctxCount = ctxData?.data?.length;
   187→
   188→  /* Current effective values */
   189→  const currentPrompt = promptDraft ?? project.agent_prompt ?? "";
   190→  const currentRules: AgentRules = rulesDraft ?? project.agent_rules ?? {};
   191→
   192→  return (
   193→    <div className="space-y-0">
   194→      {/* ===== Header zone ===== */}
   195→      <div
   196→        className="-m-6 mb-0 border-b px-6 py-5"
   197→        style={{ backgroundColor: "#ffffff", borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   198→      >
   199→        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
   200→          {/* Left: project info */}
   201→          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
   202→            <h1
   203→              className="text-[28px] font-bold leading-tight"
   204→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces), serif" }}
   205→            >
   206→              {project.name}
   207→            </h1>
   208→            {project.description && (
   209→              <p
   210→                className="mt-1 text-[14px]"
   211→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif", color: "#6b7280" }}
   212→              >
   213→                {project.description}
   214→              </p>
   215→            )}
   216→            <p
   217→              className="mt-2 text-[12px]"
   218→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   219→            >
   220→              {sourceCount !== undefined ? `${sourceCount} sources` : "-- sources"}
   221→              {" \u00B7 "}
   222→              {ctxCount !== undefined ? `${ctxCount} context sources` : "-- context sources"}
   223→            </p>
   224→          </div>
   225→
   226→          {/* Right: actions */}
   227→          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 ml-4">
   228→            <Link href={`/projects/${id}/edit`}>
   229→              <button
   230→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   231→                style={{
   232→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   233→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   234→                  color: "#374151",
   235→                }}
   236→              >
   237→                <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   238→                Edit
   239→              </button>
   240→            </Link>
   241→            <button
   242→              onClick={handleDelete}
   243→              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-red-50"
   244→              style={{
   245→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   246→                borderColor: "#dc2626",
   247→                color: "#dc2626",
   248→              }}
   249→            >
   250→              <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   251→              Delete
   252→            </button>
   253→            <button
   254→              onClick={handleTriggerRun}
   255→              disabled={triggering}
   256→              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-60"
   257→              style={{
   258→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   259→                backgroundColor: "#e8601a",
   260→              }}
   261→            >
   262→              <Play className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   263→              {triggering ? "Running..." : "Run Agent"}
   264→            </button>
   265→          </div>
   266→        </div>
   267→      </div>
   268→
   269→      {/* ===== Tab bar ===== */}
   270→      <div
   271→        className="-mx-6 border-b px-6"
   272→        style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   273→      >
   274→        <div className="flex gap-0">
   275→          {tabs.map((tab) => {
   276→            const isActive = activeTab === tab.key;
   277→            return (
   278→              <button
   279→                key={tab.key}
   280→                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.key)}
   281→                className="relative px-4 py-2.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors"
   282→                style={{
   283→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   284→                  color: isActive ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   285→                }}
   286→              >
   287→                {tab.label}
   288→                {isActive && (
   289→                  <span
   290→                    className="absolute bottom-0 left-4 right-4 h-[2px]"
   291→                    style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
   292→                  />
   293→                )}
   294→              </button>
   295→            );
   296→          })}
   297→        </div>
   298→      </div>
   299→
   300→      {/* ===== Tab content ===== */}
   301→      <div className="pt-6">
   302→        {/* --- Sources tab --- */}
   303→        {activeTab === "sources" && (
   304→          <div>
   305→            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
   306→              <SectionLabel>Sources</SectionLabel>
   307→              <Link href={`/projects/${id}/sources/new`}>
   308→                <button
   309→                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   310→                  style={{
   311→                    fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   312→                    borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   313→                    color: "#374151",
   314→                  }}
   315→                >
   316→                  <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   317→                  Add Source
   318→                </button>
   319→              </Link>
   320→            </div>
   321→
   322→            {sourcesData?.data && sourcesData.data.length > 0 ? (
   323→              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   324→                <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>
   325→                  <thead>
   326→                    <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   327→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Provider</th>
   328→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Repository</th>
   329→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Interval</th>
   330→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Status</th>
   331→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Last polled</th>
   332→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}></th>
   333→                    </tr>
   334→                  </thead>
   335→                  <tbody>
   336→                    {sourcesData.data.map((source) => (
   337→                      <tr key={source.id} className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   338→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   339→                          <ProviderBadge provider={source.provider} />
   340→                        </td>
   341→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   342→                          <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: "12px" }}>
   343→                            {source.repository}
   344→                          </span>
   345→                        </td>
   346→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>
   347→                          {formatInterval(source.poll_interval_seconds)}
   348→                        </td>
   349→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   350→                          <button
   351→                            onClick={() => handleToggleSource(source)}
   352→                            className="relative inline-flex h-5 w-9 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center rounded-full transition-colors duration-200"
   353→                            style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   354→                            title={source.enabled ? "Disable polling" : "Enable polling"}
   355→                          >
   356→                            <span
   357→                              className="inline-block h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full bg-white shadow transition-transform duration-200"
   358→                              style={{ transform: source.enabled ? "translateX(18px)" : "translateX(3px)" }}
   359→                            />
   360→                          </button>
   361→                        </td>
   362→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   363→                          {source.last_polled_at
   364→                            ? new Date(source.last_polled_at).toLocaleString()
   365→                            : "Never"}
   366→                        </td>
   367→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
   368→                          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
   369→                            <Link
   370→                              href={`/sources/${source.id}/edit`}
   371→                              className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1] hover:text-[#111113]"
   372→                              title="Edit source"
   373→                            >
   374→                              <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
   375→                            </Link>
   376→                            <button
   377→                              onClick={() => handleDeleteSource(source.id)}
   378→                              className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
   379→                              title="Delete source"
   380→                            >
   381→                              <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
   382→                            </button>
   383→                          </div>
   384→                        </td>
   385→                      </tr>
   386→                    ))}
   387→                  </tbody>
   388→                </table>
   389→              </div>
   390→            ) : (
   391→              <div
   392→                className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   393→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   394→              >
   395→                No sources configured
   396→              </div>
   397→            )}
   398→          </div>
   399→        )}
   400→
   401→        {/* --- Context Sources tab --- */}
   402→        {activeTab === "context" && (
   403→          <div>
   404→            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
   405→              <SectionLabel>Context Sources</SectionLabel>
   406→              <Link href={`/projects/${id}/context-sources/new`}>
   407→                <button
   408→                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   409→                  style={{
   410→                    fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   411→                    borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   412→                    color: "#374151",
   413→                  }}
   414→                >
   415→                  <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   416→                  Add Context Source
   417→                </button>
   418→              </Link>
   419→            </div>
   420→
   421→            {ctxData?.data && ctxData.data.length > 0 ? (
   422→              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   423→                <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>
   424→                  <thead>
   425→                    <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   426→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Type</th>
   427→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Name</th>
   428→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Config URL</th>
   429→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}></th>
   430→                    </tr>
   431→                  </thead>
   432→                  <tbody>
   433→                    {ctxData.data.map((ctx) => (
   434→                      <tr key={ctx.id} className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   435→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   436→                          <span
   437→                            className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none"
   438→                            style={{ backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1", color: "#374151" }}
   439→                          >
   440→                            {ctx.type}
   441→                          </span>
   442→                        </td>
   443→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium" style={{ color: "#111113" }}>
   444→                          {ctx.name}
   445→                        </td>
   446→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   447→                          <span
   448→                            className="text-[12px]"
   449→                            style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
   450→                            title={typeof ctx.config?.url === "string" ? ctx.config.url : ""}
   451→                          >
   452→                            {typeof ctx.config?.url === "string"
   453→                              ? truncate(ctx.config.url, 50)
   454→                              : "--"}
   455→                          </span>
   456→                        </td>
   457→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
   458→                          <button
   459→                            onClick={() => handleDeleteCtx(ctx.id)}
   460→                            className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
   461→                            title="Delete context source"
   462→                          >
   463→                            <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
   464→                          </button>
   465→                        </td>
   466→                      </tr>
   467→                    ))}
   468→                  </tbody>
   469→                </table>
   470→              </div>
   471→            ) : (
   472→              <div
   473→                className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   474→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   475→              >
   476→                No context sources configured
   477→              </div>
   478→            )}
   479→          </div>
   480→        )}
   481→
   482→        {/* --- Semantic Releases tab --- */}
   483→        {activeTab === "semantic" && (
   484→          <div>
   485→            <SectionLabel className="mb-4">Semantic Releases</SectionLabel>
   486→
   487→            {srData?.data && srData.data.length > 0 ? (
   488→              <div className="space-y-3">
   489→                {srData.data.map((sr) => (
   490→                  <Link
   491→                    key={sr.id}
   492→                    href={`/projects/${id}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}
   493→                    className="block rounded-md border p-4 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   494→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   495→                  >
   496→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
   497→                      <VersionChip version={sr.version} />
   498→                      <StatusDot status={sr.status} />
   499→                      <span className="text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>
   500→                        {sr.status}
   501→                      </span>
   502→                      {sr.report?.urgency && <UrgencyChip urgency={sr.report.urgency} />}
   503→                    </div>
   504→                    {sr.report?.summary && (
   505→                      <p
   506→                        className="mt-2 text-[13px] italic leading-relaxed"
   507→                        style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}
   508→                      >
   509→                        {truncate(sr.report.summary, 200)}
   510→                      </p>
   511→                    )}
   512→                  </Link>
   513→                ))}
   514→              </div>
   515→            ) : (
   516→              <div
   517→                className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   518→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   519→              >
   520→                No semantic releases yet
   521→              </div>
   522→            )}
   523→          </div>
   524→        )}
   525→
   526→        {/* --- Agent tab --- */}
   527→        {activeTab === "agent" && (
   528→          <div className="space-y-8">
   529→            {/* Prompt section */}
   530→            <div>
   531→              <SectionLabel className="mb-3">Agent Prompt</SectionLabel>
   532→              <textarea
   533→                value={currentPrompt}
   534→                onChange={(e) => setPromptDraft(e.target.value)}
   535→                rows={5}
   536→                placeholder="Custom instructions for the agent when analyzing releases..."
   537→                className="w-full resize-y rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-[13px] placeholder:text-[#9ca3af] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   538→                style={{
   539→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   540→                  backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   541→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   542→                  color: "#111113",
   543→                }}
   544→              />
   545→              <div className="mt-2 flex justify-end">
   546→                <button
   547→                  onClick={handleSaveAgentConfig}
   548→                  disabled={saving || (promptDraft === null && rulesDraft === null)}
   549→                  className="rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   550→                  style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
   551→                >
   552→                  {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}
   553→                </button>
   554→              </div>
   555→            </div>
   556→
   557→            {/* Rules section */}
   558→            <div>
   559→              <SectionLabel className="mb-3">Trigger Rules</SectionLabel>
   560→              <div className="space-y-3">
   561→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   562→                  <input
   563→                    type="checkbox"
   564→                    checked={currentRules.on_major_release ?? false}
   565→                    onChange={(e) =>
   566→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_major_release: e.target.checked })
   567→                    }
   568→                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border accent-[#e8601a]"
   569→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   570→                  />
   571→                  On Major Release
   572→                </label>
   573→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   574→                  <input
   575→                    type="checkbox"
   576→                    checked={currentRules.on_minor_release ?? false}
   577→                    onChange={(e) =>
   578→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_minor_release: e.target.checked })
   579→                    }
   580→                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border accent-[#e8601a]"
   581→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   582→                  />
   583→                  On Minor Release
   584→                </label>
   585→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   586→                  <input
   587→                    type="checkbox"
   588→                    checked={currentRules.on_security_patch ?? false}
   589→                    onChange={(e) =>
   590→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_security_patch: e.target.checked })
   591→                    }
   592→                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border accent-[#e8601a]"
   593→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   594→                  />
   595→                  On Security Patch
   596→                </label>
   597→                <div className="mt-4">
   598→                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   599→                    Version Pattern
   600→                  </label>
   601→                  <input
   602→                    type="text"
   603→                    value={currentRules.version_pattern ?? ""}
   604→                    onChange={(e) =>
   605→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, version_pattern: e.target.value })
   606→                    }
   607→                    placeholder="e.g. ^v\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+$"
   608→                    className="w-full max-w-md rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] placeholder:text-[#9ca3af] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   609→                    style={{
   610→                      fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   611→                      backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   612→                      borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   613→                      color: "#111113",
   614→                    }}
   615→                  />
   616→                </div>
   617→              </div>
   618→            </div>
   619→
   620→            {/* Run History section */}
   621→            <div>

   625→                <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   626→                  <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>

   630→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Status</th>

   635→                    </thead>

   645→                              <span style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>{run.status}</span>

   649→                            {run.started_at

   657→                            {run.semantic_release_id ? (

   659→                                href={`/projects/${id}/semantic-releases/${run.semantic_release_id}`}
   660→                                className="text-[13px] font-medium underline"
   661→                                style={{ color: "#e8601a" }}
   662→                              >
   663→                                View
   664→                              </Link>
   665→                            ) : (
   666→                              <span style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>--</span>
   667→                            )}
   668→                          </td>
   669→                        </tr>
   670→                      ))}
   671→                    </tbody>
   672→                  </table>
   673→                </div>
   674→              ) : (
   675→                <div
   676→                  className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   677→                  style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   678→                >
   679→                  No agent runs yet
   680→                </div>
   681→              )}
   682→            </div>
   683→          </div>
   684→        )}
   685→      </div>
   686→    </div>
   687→  );

}
